```java
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.FightLevel;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.world.ArenaRun;

/**
 * R1.4: a ring of dirt dodged every bomb in 1.3.0, so kids in a fight are in adventure mode. Their own mode
 * comes back at the end of the fight, when they leave the arena, when they die, and when they log in again.
 */
public class FightModeGameTest {
	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void aKidInAFightCantBreakOrPlace(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		BlockPos block = arena.kid.blockPosition().east(3);
		arena.level.setBlockAndUpdate(block, Blocks.DIRT.defaultBlockState());

		helper.runAfterDelay(3, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(mode(arena.kid) == GameType.ADVENTURE, "adventure mode while the fight runs, not " + mode(arena.kid));
			helper.assertFalse(arena.kid.getAbilities().mayBuild, "so he may not build");
			helper.assertFalse(arena.kid.gameMode.destroyBlock(block), "and breaking a block does nothing");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.level.getBlockState(block).is(Blocks.DIRT), "the block is still there");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void theirOwnModeComesBackWhenTheFightEnds(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		arena.kid.setGameMode(GameType.SURVIVAL);

		helper.runAfterDelay(3, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(mode(arena.kid) == GameType.ADVENTURE, "adventure in the fight");
			arena.end();
			helper.assertTrue(mode(arena.kid) == GameType.SURVIVAL, "survival again, the mode he came with: " + mode(arena.kid));
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void aKidWhoLeavesTheArenaGetsTheirOwnModeBack(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		helper.runAfterDelay(3, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(mode(arena.kid) == GameType.ADVENTURE, "adventure in the fight");
			arena.kid.teleportTo(arena.box.minX() - 15, arena.floorY, arena.box.middleZ());
		});
		helper.runAfterDelay(8, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(mode(arena.kid) == GameType.CREATIVE, "creative again, the mode a mock kid comes with: " + mode(arena.kid));
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 60, padding = 48)
	public void aKidWhoDiesGetsTheirOwnModeBack(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		ArenaFight.survival(arena.kid);
		helper.runAfterDelay(3, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(mode(arena.kid) == GameType.ADVENTURE, "adventure in the fight");
			arena.kid.kill(arena.level);
		});
		helper.runAfterDelay(12, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.kid.isDeadOrDying(), "the kid died");
			helper.assertTrue(mode(arena.kid) == GameType.SURVIVAL, "survival again, so a respawn is not in adventure mode: " + mode(arena.kid));
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void aKidWhoLogsInStillInAdventureModeGetsTheirOwnModeBack(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		// Not in the fight: a kid who was saved in adventure mode when the game stopped mid-fight.
		ServerPlayer saved = arena.addKid(arena.box.middleX() + 15, arena.box.middleZ());
		saved.setGameMode(GameType.SURVIVAL);
		saved.setGameMode(GameType.ADVENTURE);

		helper.runAfterDelay(3, () -> {
			arena.fights.onJoin(saved);
			helper.assertTrue(mode(saved) == GameType.SURVIVAL, "back to the mode before: " + mode(saved));
			arena.fights.onJoin(arena.kid);
			helper.assertTrue(mode(arena.kid) == GameType.ADVENTURE, "a kid who is in the running fight is left alone");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void withNoBuildInFightOffTheKidKeepsTheirOwnMode(GameTestHelper helper) {
		TafelBoemConfig config = new TafelBoemConfig();
		config.noBuildInFight = false;
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, config);

		helper.runAfterDelay(3, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(mode(arena.kid) == GameType.CREATIVE, "no switch, no adventure mode: " + mode(arena.kid));
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = TestWorld.PADDING)
	@Fight
	public void aFightStartsWithoutABuildPhaseAndTheKidIsBackInTheirModeOnLeaving(GameTestHelper helper) {
		TestWorld test = new TestWorld(helper).running();
		ServerPlayer kid = test.kidInTheHub();
		test.world.enterDoor(kid, 1);
		boolean[] leaving = {false};

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			if (!leaving[0]) {
				ArenaRun run = test.world.run(FightLevel.MAKKELIJK).orElse(null);
				helper.assertTrue(run != null && run.fight() != null, "wait for him: about 3 s after the kid came in, as in 1.1.2");
				helper.assertTrue(run.building() == null, "no build phase");
				helper.assertTrue(mode(kid) == GameType.ADVENTURE, "adventure in the fight: " + mode(kid));
				// The exit door. With the only kid gone the run ends, so from here on only his mode is checked.
				test.world.enterDoor(kid, 0);
				leaving[0] = true;
			}
			helper.assertTrue(mode(kid) == GameType.CREATIVE, "back to creative once he has left: " + mode(kid));
			test.close();
		});
	}

	private static GameType mode(ServerPlayer kid) {
		return kid.gameMode.getGameModeForPlayer();
	}
}
```

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.boss;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.level.GameType;

/**
 * No breaking or placing in a fight (R1.4): a ring of dirt dodged every bomb in 1.3.0. Kids in the arena of a
 * running fight are in adventure mode; their own mode comes back when the fight ends, they leave the arena, die
 * or go offline. A kid who is already in adventure mode or spectating is left alone, and nothing is remembered
 * for them.
 */
final class FightGameModes {
	private final Map<UUID, GameType> own = new HashMap<>();

	/** Once a tick: every kid in the fight goes to adventure mode; every kid who left, died or went offline gets their own mode back. */
	void update(MinecraftServer server, List<ServerPlayer> inFight) {
		for (ServerPlayer kid : inFight) {
			GameType mode = kid.gameMode.getGameModeForPlayer();
			if (kid.isAlive() && !own.containsKey(kid.getUUID()) && (mode == GameType.SURVIVAL || mode == GameType.CREATIVE)) {
				own.put(kid.getUUID(), mode);
				kid.setGameMode(GameType.ADVENTURE);
			}
		}
		for (UUID id : List.copyOf(own.keySet())) {
			ServerPlayer kid = server.getPlayerList().getPlayer(id);
			if (kid == null) {
				own.remove(id);
			} else if (!kid.isAlive() || !inFight.contains(kid)) {
				restore(kid);
			}
		}
	}

	/** The fight is over. */
	void restoreAll(MinecraftServer server) {
		for (UUID id : List.copyOf(own.keySet())) {
			ServerPlayer kid = server.getPlayerList().getPlayer(id);
			if (kid == null) {
				own.remove(id);
			} else {
				restore(kid);
			}
		}
	}

	private void restore(ServerPlayer kid) {
		GameType mode = own.remove(kid.getUUID());
		// Only from adventure: a kid the parent put in another mode meanwhile keeps that one.
		if (mode != null && kid.gameMode.getGameModeForPlayer() == GameType.ADVENTURE) {
			kid.setGameMode(mode);
		}
	}

	/**
	 * A kid who logs in while no fight holds them but is in adventure mode was saved there by a fight that never got
	 * to end (a crash, the process killed): Minecraft saved the mode before that as the previous one, or the
	 * server's default stands in.
	 */
	static void afterLogin(ServerPlayer kid) {
		if (kid.gameMode.getGameModeForPlayer() != GameType.ADVENTURE) {
			return;
		}
		GameType before = kid.gameMode.getPreviousGameModeForPlayer();
		kid.setGameMode(before != null && before != GameType.ADVENTURE ? before : kid.level().getServer().getDefaultGameType());
	}
}
```

`src/gametest/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/gametest/BookkeepingGameTest.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.gametest;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTestHelper;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;

import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.gametest.v1.GameTest;

import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.TafelBoem;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.boss.ThrownBomb;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.config.TafelBoemConfig;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.BombTier;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.Outcome;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.YeetStyle;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.game.QuizListener;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.game.QuizSession;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.net.QuizS2C;

/**
 * R1.4: two questions at once is normal play, so the fight keeps who answers what. A yeet credits the kid whose
 * answer started it, a fight that ends closes every open question, and a question that ends without an answer
 * tells its fight.
 */
public class BookkeepingGameTest {
	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aYeetCreditsTheKidWhoseAnswerStartedItEvenWhenTheSiblingAnswersWhileHeFlies(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		ServerPlayer sibling = arena.addKid(arena.box.middleX() + 7, arena.box.middleZ() + 5);
		int startHits = arena.fight.hitsRemaining();
		// Before his first throw (60 ticks): the sibling is already answering, so his bomb comes for the other kid.
		helper.runAfterDelay(5, () -> arena.fight.placeLyingBomb(sibling.blockPosition().east(), BombTier.GEWONE_TNT));

		boolean[] bothAsked = {false};
		boolean[] siblingAnswered = {false};
		boolean[] firstOwnsHisFlight = {false};
		boolean[] stillTheFirstAfterTheSiblingAnswered = {false};
		boolean[] siblingOwnsTheNext = {false};
		Set<ServerPlayer> answered = new HashSet<>();
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			bothAsked[0] |= arena.sessions.isBusy(arena.kid) && arena.sessions.isBusy(sibling);
			if (!bothAsked[0]) {
				return;
			}
			// Both questions are open: the thrown kid answers now, the sibling once that kick has hit and he is flying.
			if (answered.add(arena.kid)) {
				arena.answerRight(arena.kid, arena.questionsTo(arena.kid).getFirst());
			}
			if (arena.fight.hitsRemaining() == startHits - 1 && !siblingAnswered[0]) {
				siblingAnswered[0] = true;
				firstOwnsHisFlight[0] = arena.kid.getUUID().equals(arena.fight.creditedKid());
				arena.answerRight(sibling, arena.questionsTo(sibling).getFirst());
				// The sibling's answer used to overwrite the one shared kick, so the first kid's flight got credited to them.
				stillTheFirstAfterTheSiblingAnswered[0] = arena.kid.getUUID().equals(arena.fight.creditedKid());
			}
			if (arena.fight.hitsRemaining() == startHits - 2) {
				siblingOwnsTheNext[0] = sibling.getUUID().equals(arena.fight.creditedKid());
			}
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(siblingAnswered[0], "wait for both kicks");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.hitsRemaining() == startHits - 2, "wait until the sibling's kick has hit too");
			helper.assertTrue(firstOwnsHisFlight[0], "his first flight is the first kid's");
			helper.assertTrue(stillTheFirstAfterTheSiblingAnswered[0], "the sibling's answer doesn't take over the first kid's flight");
			helper.assertTrue(siblingOwnsTheNext[0], "once the sibling's kick has hit, the new flight is the sibling's");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 300, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aFightThatEndsWithAQuestionOpenClosesIt(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		int[] openSession = {-1};

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			if (openSession[0] < 0) {
				helper.assertTrue(arena.sessions.isBusy(arena.kid), "wait for his first question");
				openSession[0] = arena.questionsTo(arena.kid).getFirst().sessionId();
				arena.end();
			}
			helper.assertFalse(arena.sessions.isBusy(arena.kid), "the question is closed with the fight");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.client.sentTo.stream().anyMatch(sent -> sent.player() == arena.kid
					&& sent.payload().mode() == QuizS2C.CLOSE && sent.payload().sessionId() == openSession[0]), "and its screen is told to close");
			helper.assertFalse(arena.kid.getAttribute(Attributes.MOVEMENT_SPEED).hasModifier(TafelBoem.id("rooted")), "the kid can walk again");
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void aQuestionThatEndsWhenTheKidDisconnectsTellsItsFight(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		Ended listener = new Ended();
		arena.sessions.start(arena.kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE, arena.kid.blockPosition().east(), "test", listener);

		arena.sessions.onDisconnect(arena.kid);

		helper.assertTrue(listener.ended.equals(List.of(arena.kid.getUUID())), "the owner was told once: " + listener.ended);
		helper.assertFalse(arena.sessions.isBusy(arena.kid), "and the question is gone");
		arena.end();
		helper.succeed();
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void aQuestionThatEndsWhenTheKidDiesTellsItsFight(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		ArenaFight.survival(arena.kid);
		Ended listener = new Ended();
		arena.sessions.start(arena.kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE, arena.kid.blockPosition().east(), "test", listener);
		arena.kid.kill(arena.level);

		helper.runAfterDelay(5, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(listener.ended.equals(List.of(arena.kid.getUUID())), "the owner was told once: " + listener.ended);
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void aQuestionThatTimesOutTellsItsFight(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		Ended listener = new Ended();
		arena.sessions.start(arena.kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE, arena.kid.blockPosition().east(), "test", listener);

		// SessionManager.ANSWER_TIMEOUT_TICKS is 2400: tick it that far in one go instead of waiting two minutes.
		for (int i = 0; i < 2500; i++) {
			arena.sessions.tick();
		}

		helper.assertTrue(listener.ended.equals(List.of(arena.kid.getUUID())), "the owner was told once: " + listener.ended);
		helper.assertFalse(arena.sessions.isBusy(arena.kid), "the question is gone");
		arena.end();
		helper.succeed();
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 300, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void heNeverThrowsWhileHeFliesOrLands(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		AABB around = new AABB(arena.box.minX(), arena.box.minY(), arena.box.minZ(), arena.box.maxX() + 1, arena.box.maxY() + 40,
				arena.box.maxZ() + 1);
		boolean[] reacted = {false};
		int[] thrownWhileReacting = {0};
		// A helicopter flight and its landing take longer than the 60 ticks before his first throw.
		helper.runAfterDelay(5, () -> arena.fight.boss().yeet(arena.kid.position(), 1.0, YeetStyle.HELICOPTER, arena.kid.position()));
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			if (arena.fight.boss().isReacting()) {
				reacted[0] = true;
				thrownWhileReacting[0] += arena.level.getEntitiesOfClass(ThrownBomb.class, around).size();
			}
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(reacted[0] && !arena.fight.boss().isReacting(), "wait until his flight and landing are over");
			helper.assertTrue(thrownWhileReacting[0] == 0, "he threw " + thrownWhileReacting[0] + " bomb-ticks while he flew or landed");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	/** Remembers every question that ended without an answer. */
	private static final class Ended implements QuizListener {
		final List<UUID> ended = new ArrayList<>();

		@Override
		public void onCorrect(ServerPlayer player, QuizSession session) {
		}

		@Override
		public void onMiss(ServerPlayer player, QuizSession session, Outcome outcome) {
		}

		@Override
		public void onEnded(UUID player, QuizSession session) {
			ended.add(player);
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The question kinds and the listener call**

`src/main/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/boss/QuestionKind.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.boss;

import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.YeetKind;

/**
 * What an open question of a fight is for (R1.4), kept per kid in {@code BossFight}: a right answer on a bomb
 * he threw kicks it back, on a Terug! question it blows up on him, on a juggle question it super-yeets him. The
 * context is what the answer log says, so Terug! sums can be read apart.
 */
enum QuestionKind {
	/** A bomb he threw at the kid. */
	BOMB("fight", YeetKind.KICK),
	/** A Terugbom the kid threw back hit him. */
	TERUG("terug", YeetKind.BACK),
	/** A Terugbom the kid threw back hit him in mid-yeet: he hangs frozen until this question ends. */
	JUGGLE("juggle", YeetKind.SUPER);

	private final String context;
	private final YeetKind yeetKind;

	QuestionKind(String context, YeetKind yeetKind) {
		this.context = context;
		this.yeetKind = yeetKind;
	}

	String context() {
		return context;
	}

	/** What a right answer to this question yeets him with. */
	YeetKind yeetKind() {
		return yeetKind;
	}
}
```

```java
	 * The question ended without an answer: it timed out, or the kid died or disconnected (R1.4). A fight that
	 * closes its own questions with {@code SessionManager.close} is not told.
	 */
```

```java
		if (session != null) {
			RootLock.release(player);
			notifyEnded(player.getUUID(), session);
```


```java
	/** The question goes without an answer: no pin, no bomb, no screen, and its fight hears of it. */
	private void endQuestion(Iterator<QuizSession> it, ServerPlayer player, QuizSession session) {
		it.remove();
		RootLock.release(player);
		BombEffects.fizzle(player.level(), session.bombPos());
		client.send(player, QuizS2C.close(session.id()));
		notifyEnded(player.getUUID(), session);
	}

	private void notifyEnded(UUID player, QuizSession session) {
		QuizListener listener = session.listener();
		if (listener != null) {
			listener.onEnded(player, session);
		}
	}

	/**
	 * Closes this kid's question without an answer, for whoever owns it (a fight that is over): no pin, no screen, the
	 * bomb fizzles. The owner is not told: it asked for this.
	 */
	public void close(UUID player) {
		QuizSession session = sessions.remove(player);
		if (session == null) {
			return;
		}
		ServerPlayer kid = server.getPlayerList().getPlayer(player);
		if (kid != null) {
			RootLock.release(kid);
			BombEffects.fizzle(kid.level(), session.bombPos());
			client.send(kid, QuizS2C.close(session.id()));
		}
	}
```

```java
	/** A bomb landed on its kid: they are rooted and the question comes. */
	private void root(ServerPlayer target, BlockPos pos, BombTier tier) {
		level.setBlock(pos, ModBlocks.bomb(tier).defaultBlockState(), Block.UPDATE_ALL);
		if (ask(target, tier, pos, QuestionKind.BOMB) == null) {
			BombEffects.fizzle(level, pos);
			cooldown(COOLDOWN_TICKS);
			return;
		}
		// Not while he flies or lands from a kick that came meanwhile: that reaction keeps its phase.
		if (phase == Phase.COOLDOWN || phase == Phase.BOMB_IN_FLIGHT || phase == Phase.ANSWERING) {
			phase = Phase.ANSWERING;
		}
	}

	/** Asks this kid a question about a bomb; it stays open until it is answered or ends, and the fight remembers what it is for. */
	private @Nullable QuizSession ask(ServerPlayer kid, BombTier tier, BlockPos at, QuestionKind kind) {
		QuizSession session = sessions.start(kid, tier, at, kind.context(), this);
		if (session != null) {
			open.put(kid.getUUID(), kind);
			sessions.client().track(kid, boss, 0, false);
		}
		return session;
	}
```

```java
	@Override
	public void onCorrect(ServerPlayer player, QuizSession session) {
		open.remove(player.getUUID());
		FightTally tally = tally(player.getUUID());
		tally.record(Outcome.CORRECT);
		BlockPos pos = session.bombPos();
		if (level.getBlockState(pos).getBlock() instanceof BombBlock) {
			level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), Block.UPDATE_ALL);
		}
		// The kick rides on the bomb, so a second answer meanwhile can't change who gets this yeet.
		Kick kick = new Kick(player.getUUID(), session.fact(), session.tier(), session.answerMillis(), session.usualMillis(), session.wasFast(),
				tally.streak());
		ThrownBomb thrown = ThrownBomb.kicked(level, player, session.tier(), this, boss, kick);
		thrown.setPos(Vec3.atCenterOf(pos));
		level.addFreshEntity(thrown);

		// The simple kick: a vanilla swing everyone sees, a whoosh, and a camera bump on the kid's screen.
		player.swing(InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND, true);
		level.playSound(null, pos, SoundEvents.PLAYER_ATTACK_SWEEP, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.2F, 0.8F);
		sessions.client().track(player, boss, KICK_CAM_MAX_TICKS, true);

		if (!decided()) {
			phase = Phase.KICK_IN_FLIGHT;
			phaseTicks = FLIGHT_TIMEOUT;
		}
	}
```

```java
	void onKickHit(Vec3 where, BombTier tier, @Nullable Kick kick) {
		if (decided()) {
			return;
		}
		// The bigger the bomb, the bigger the bang and the further he flies.
		boom(new Vec3(boss.getX(), boss.getY() + 1, boss.getZ()), tier);
		if (tier == BombTier.KIPPENBOM && config.kippenbomChickens) {
			if (config.countingKippenbom && kick != null) {
				sessions.countChickens(level, boss.position(), kick.fact(), "fight");
			} else {
				BombEffects.flyingChickens(level, boss.position());
				sessions.collectSpectacles(level, boss.blockPosition());
			}
		}
		if (hit()) {
			return;
		}

		ServerPlayer kicker = kick == null ? null : level.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayer(kick.kicker());
		Vec3 kickedFrom = kicker != null ? kicker.position() : where;
		yeetKick = kick;
		yeetFactor = kick == null ? 1.0 : kick.factor(config.yeetBoost);
		double power = (0.9 + level.getRandom().nextDouble() * 0.5) * tier.yeetPower() * yeetFactor;
		reactionCooldown = COOLDOWN_TICKS;
		boss.yeet(where, power, reactions.nextFlight(arena != null, config.yeetCatalogue), kickedFrom, traps());
		phase = Phase.REACTING;
		phaseTicks = REACTION_MIN_TICKS;
	}
```

```java
	/** Kicked back at the boss by a player who answered correctly; the kick rides along, so it can't be mixed up with another. */
	static ThrownBomb kicked(ServerLevel level, LivingEntity kicker, BombTier tier, BossFight fight, GraafFout boss, Kick kick) {
		ThrownBomb bomb = new ThrownBomb(ModEntities.THROWN_BOMB, level);
		bomb.setItem(new ItemStack(ModBlocks.bombItem(tier)));
		bomb.setOwner(kicker);
		bomb.tier = tier;
		bomb.fight = fight;
		bomb.kickTarget = boss;
		bomb.kick = kick;
		bomb.setNoGravity(true);
		return bomb;
	}
```

```java
	private final Map<ServerPlayer, double[]> walking = new HashMap<>();

	ArenaFight(GameTestHelper helper, TafelBoemConfig config) {
		this(helper, config, FightLevel.MAKKELIJK, true);
	}

	/**
	 * @param inArena false: he is summoned with no arena, as the summon item does outside a TafelBoem world;
	 *                {@link #box} then only says where the 21 × 21 floor is
	 */
	ArenaFight(GameTestHelper helper, TafelBoemConfig config, boolean inArena) {
		this(helper, config, FightLevel.MAKKELIJK, inArena);
	}

	/** @param fightLevel the level he fights at: its bombs, its tables and its aim */
	@SuppressWarnings("removal") // mock players are still the only way to test without a client
	ArenaFight(GameTestHelper helper, TafelBoemConfig config, FightLevel fightLevel, boolean inArena) {
		OpenField.open(helper);
		this.helper = helper;
		this.level = helper.getLevel();
		this.sessions = new SessionManager(level.getServer(), new Learners(new AnswerLog(tempDir())), client, config);
		this.fights = new BossFights(sessions);
		// OpenField's stone floor is one block below the test's corner: that is the arena's bottom.
		BlockPos corner = helper.absolutePos(new BlockPos(-10, -1, -10));
		this.box = ArenaBox.of(corner.getX(), corner.getY(), corner.getZ(), 21, 6, 21);
		this.floorY = corner.getY() + 1;
		this.kid = helper.makeMockServerPlayerInLevel();
		kid.teleportTo(box.middleX(), floorY, box.middleZ() + 5);
		BossFight summoned = fights.summon(level, new Vec3(box.middleX(), floorY + 1.5, box.middleZ()), fightLevel, inArena ? box : null);
		helper.assertTrue(summoned != null, "Graaf Fout should appear");
		this.fight = summoned;
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			walking.forEach((walker, step) -> walker.teleportTo(walker.getX() + step[0], walker.getY(), walker.getZ() + step[1]));
			sessions.tick();
			fights.tick();
		});
	}

	/** From now on this kid walks this far every tick (a mock player is moved by teleporting it once a tick); (0, 0) stops them. */
	void walk(ServerPlayer kid, double dx, double dz) {
		walking.put(kid, new double[] {dx, dz});
	}

	/** His bombs in the air over this arena now: not the kicks and the throw-backs of the kids, which have a kid for an owner. */
	List<ThrownBomb> bombsInTheAir() {
		return level.getEntitiesOfClass(ThrownBomb.class,
				new AABB(box.minX(), box.minY(), box.minZ(), box.maxX() + 1, box.maxY() + 40, box.maxZ() + 1),
				bomb -> bomb.getOwner() == fight.boss());
	}
```

`src/gametest/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/gametest/AimGameTest.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.gametest;

import java.util.List;

import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTestHelper;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.gametest.v1.GameTest;

import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.config.TafelBoemConfig;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.Aim;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.FightLevel;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.FightTally;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.Pacing;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.ThrowReport;

/**
 * R1.4's aim: he throws where a running kid will be, so a kid who runs straight is hit, a kid who turns after the
 * throw dodges, a kid who stands still is always hit, and after a dodge his next throw is 90 ticks away.
 */
public class AimGameTest {
	/** 4.3 blocks a second: a kid walking. */
	private static final double WALKING = 0.216;

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aKidWhoStandsStillIsHitOnLevel1(GameTestHelper helper) {
		standingKidIsHit(helper, FightLevel.MAKKELIJK);
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aKidWhoStandsStillIsHitOnLevel2(GameTestHelper helper) {
		standingKidIsHit(helper, FightLevel.GEMIDDELD);
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aKidWhoStandsStillIsHitOnLevel3(GameTestHelper helper) {
		standingKidIsHit(helper, FightLevel.MOEILIJK);
	}

	private static void standingKidIsHit(GameTestHelper helper, FightLevel level) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig(), level, true);

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			FightTally tally = arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID());
			helper.assertTrue(tally.thrownAt() >= 1, "wait for his first throw to land");
			helper.assertTrue(tally.dodged() == 0, "a kid who stands still is always hit");
			helper.assertTrue(tally.still() >= 1, "and counts as standing still");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.askedAnything(), "so the question comes");
			ThrowReport line = arena.fight.lastThrow();
			helper.assertTrue(line != null && line.level() == level.number() && line.hit() && line.still(), "the throw line says so: " + line);
			helper.assertTrue(line.flightTicks() >= Aim.MIN_FLIGHT_TICKS, "and the flight was at least 12 ticks: " + line);
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aKidRunningStraightAtWalkingSpeedIsHitOnLevel3(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig(), FightLevel.MOEILIJK, true);
		// From the west side of the floor towards the east: across his line of fire, the way a kid dodges.
		arena.kid.teleportTo(arena.box.middleX() - 9, arena.floorY, arena.box.middleZ() + 5);
		arena.walk(arena.kid, WALKING, 0);

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			FightTally tally = arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID());
			helper.assertTrue(tally.thrownAt() >= 1, "wait for his first throw to land");
			helper.assertTrue(tally.dodged() == 0, "he aimed where the kid was going: a hit");
			helper.assertTrue(tally.still() == 0, "and the kid was moving: " + arena.fight.lastThrow());
			helper.assertTrue(arena.askedAnything(), "so the question comes");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aKidWhoTurnsAfterTheThrowDodges(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig(), FightLevel.MOEILIJK, true);
		arena.kid.teleportTo(arena.box.middleX() - 9, arena.floorY, arena.box.middleZ() + 5);
		arena.walk(arena.kid, WALKING, 0);
		boolean[] turned = {false};
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			// His bomb is in the air, aimed ahead: now the kid turns round.
			if (!turned[0] && !arena.bombsInTheAir().isEmpty()) {
				turned[0] = true;
				arena.walk(arena.kid, -WALKING, 0);
			}
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			FightTally tally = arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID());
			helper.assertTrue(tally.thrownAt() >= 1, "wait for his first throw to land");
			helper.assertTrue(tally.dodged() == 1, "a kid who turned is dodged: " + arena.fight.lastThrow());
			helper.assertFalse(arena.askedAnything(), "so no question");
			helper.assertFalse(arena.sessions.isBusy(arena.kid), "and nobody is rooted");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 500, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void afterADodgeHisNextThrowComes90TicksLater(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig(), FightLevel.MOEILIJK, true);
		arena.kid.teleportTo(arena.box.middleX() - 9, arena.floorY, arena.box.middleZ() + 5);
		arena.walk(arena.kid, WALKING, 0);
		long[] dodgedAt = {-1};
		long[] nextThrowAt = {-1};
		boolean[] turned = {false};
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			if (!turned[0] && !arena.bombsInTheAir().isEmpty()) {
				turned[0] = true;
				arena.walk(arena.kid, -WALKING, 0);
			}
			if (dodgedAt[0] < 0 && arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).dodged() == 1) {
				dodgedAt[0] = helper.getTick();
				arena.walk(arena.kid, 0, 0);
			} else if (dodgedAt[0] >= 0 && nextThrowAt[0] < 0 && !arena.bombsInTheAir().isEmpty()) {
				nextThrowAt[0] = helper.getTick();
			}
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(nextThrowAt[0] >= 0, "wait for his next throw");
			long wait = nextThrowAt[0] - dodgedAt[0];
			helper.assertTrue(Math.abs(wait - Pacing.AFTER_DODGE_COOLDOWN_TICKS) <= 2, "his next throw came " + wait + " ticks after the dodge, not 90");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void theLandingRingMarksWhereTheBombWillLand(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertFalse(arena.bombsInTheAir().isEmpty(), "wait for his first throw");
			List<Vec3> rings = arena.fight.landingRings();
			helper.assertTrue(rings.size() == 1, "one ring while the bomb flies: " + rings);
			Vec3 ring = rings.getFirst();
			helper.assertTrue(Math.abs(ring.x - arena.kid.getX()) <= 0.71 && Math.abs(ring.z - arena.kid.getZ()) <= 0.71,
					"centred on the block where the standing kid is: " + ring + " against " + arena.kid.position());
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void withTheRingSwitchedOffNoRingShows(GameTestHelper helper) {
		TafelBoemConfig config = new TafelBoemConfig();
		config.landingRing = false;
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, config);

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertFalse(arena.bombsInTheAir().isEmpty(), "wait for his first throw");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.landingRings().isEmpty(), "with the switch off there is no ring");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void withDodgingOffHeAimsAsInOnePointTwoAndWritesNoThrowLine(GameTestHelper helper) {
		TafelBoemConfig config = new TafelBoemConfig();
		config.dodging = false;
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, config);
		boolean[] ringSeen = {false};
		helper.onEachTick(() -> ringSeen[0] |= !arena.fight.landingRings().isEmpty());

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.askedAnything(), "wait for the question: wherever it lands, its kid answers");
			helper.assertFalse(ringSeen[0], "no ring");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.lastThrow() == null, "no throw line");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).thrownAt() == 0, "and nothing counted");
			arena.end();
		});
	}
}
```

New methods:

```java
	private void recordMotions() {
		for (ServerPlayer kid : participants.players()) {
			motions.computeIfAbsent(kid.getUUID(), id -> new Motion()).record(kid.getX(), kid.getZ());
		}
	}

	/** Every other tick a ring of dust at the radius just inside the blast zone, in the colour of the bomb's trail. */
	private void showRings() {
		rings.removeIf(ring -> --ring.ticksLeft < 0);
		if (level.getGameTime() % 2 != 0) {
			return;
		}
		for (Ring ring : rings) {
			DustParticleOptions dust = new DustParticleOptions(ring.tier.trailColor(), 1.0F);
			for (int i = 0; i < RING_POINTS; i++) {
				double[] point = Dodging.ringPoint(ring.centre.x, ring.centre.z, i, RING_POINTS);
				level.sendParticles(dust, point[0], ring.centre.y + 0.1, point[1], 1, 0, 0, 0, 0);
			}
		}
	}

	/** The rings showing now: the middles of the landing blocks he aimed at. */
	public List<Vec3> landingRings() {
		return rings.stream().map(ring -> ring.centre).toList();
	}

	/**
	 * A ring shows around this spot for this many ticks, or until a bomb lands. His own throws call it, for the flight of
	 * the bomb; the GameTests and the client test put one where they need it.
	 */
	public void showLandingRing(Vec3 centre, BombTier tier, int ticks) {
		rings.add(new Ring(centre, tier, ticks));
	}

	/** The last "TafelBoem throw:" line this fight wrote, or null before the first; for the GameTests. */
	public @Nullable ThrowReport lastThrow() {
		return lastThrow;
	}
```

A ring goes when its time is up or when a bomb lands (`onBombLanded` clears them).

Replace `throwVelocity` and its old Javadoc by the two overloads (the first keeps the 1.2.0 flight time, from today's formula in `Aim.baseTicks`):

```java
	/** A drag-free parabola with today's flight time: each tick vy -= g, then pos += v. Lands on {@code to}. */
	static Vec3 throwVelocity(Vec3 from, Vec3 to, double gravity) {
		Vec3 delta = to.subtract(from);
		return throwVelocity(from, to, gravity, Aim.baseTicks(Math.sqrt(delta.x * delta.x + delta.z * delta.z)));
	}

	/** The same parabola, landing on {@code to} after exactly {@code ticks}. */
	static Vec3 throwVelocity(Vec3 from, Vec3 to, double gravity, int ticks) {
		Vec3 delta = to.subtract(from);
		double vy = (delta.y + gravity * ticks * (ticks + 1) / 2.0) / ticks;
		return new Vec3(delta.x / ticks, vy, delta.z / ticks);
	}
```

```java
			// R1.4: where the kid will be when the bomb lands, times the lead of this level, in a flight of the level's time.
			Motion motion = motions.computeIfAbsent(target.getUUID(), id -> new Motion());
			Aim.Plan plan = Aim.plan(boss.getX(), boss.getZ(), target.getX(), target.getZ(), motion.velX(), motion.velZ(),
					config.aimFor(fightLevel), arena);
			Vec3 aim = new Vec3(plan.x(), target.getY(), plan.z());
			ThrowInfo info = new ThrowInfo(Math.hypot(aim.x - boss.getX(), aim.z - boss.getZ()), plan.ticks(), motion.speed(), target.getX(),
					target.getZ());
			bomb = ThrownBomb.thrown(level, boss, tier, this, target.getUUID(), info);
			bomb.setDeltaMovement(throwVelocity(start, aim, bomb.getGravity(), plan.ticks()));
			if (config.landingRing) {
				BlockPos block = BlockPos.containing(aim);
				showLandingRing(new Vec3(block.getX() + 0.5, aim.y, block.getZ() + 0.5), tier, plan.ticks() + 2);

			bomb = ThrownBomb.thrown(level, boss, tier, this, target.getUUID(), null);
			Vec3 inFront = target.position().add(boss.position().subtract(target.position()).multiply(1, 0, 1).normalize().scale(1.2));
			bomb.setDeltaMovement(throwVelocity(start, inFront, bomb.getGravity()));
		}

		level.addFreshEntity(bomb);
```

Replace `onBombLanded` and add `countThrow`:

```java
	void onBombLanded(ServerLevel level, BlockPos landing, BombTier tier, UUID targetId, @Nullable ThrowInfo info) {
		rings.clear();
		ServerPlayer target = level.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayer(targetId);
		BlockPos pos = findAir(level, landing);
		// Nowhere to put it, or he was beaten while it was in the air: it poofs.
		if (pos == null || decided()) {
			poofAndPause(landing);
			return;
		}
		if (!dodging()) {
			// 1.2.0: wherever it lands, its kid answers, as long as they are there and free.
			if (target != null && !sessions.isBusy(target)) {
				root(target, pos, tier);
			} else {
				poofAndPause(landing);
			}
			return;
		}
		boolean free = target != null && target.isAlive() && !sessions.isBusy(target) && participants.players().contains(target);
		boolean hit = free && Dodging.isHit(pos.getX() + 0.5, pos.getZ() + 0.5, target.getX(), target.getZ());
		if (free) {
			countThrow(target, info, hit);
		}
		if (hit) {
			root(target, pos, tier);
			return;
		}
		// The kid ran, or a block stopped it; also for a kid who has gone, died or is answering another bomb.
		placeLyingBomb(restingSpot(pos), tier);
		if (free) {
			participants.sayTo(target, Component.translatable("tafelboem.boss.graaf_fout.dodged"));
		}
		// Time to catch it, turn round and throw it back.
		cooldown(Pacing.AFTER_DODGE_COOLDOWN_TICKS);
	}

	/** A throw at a free kid has landed: the tally, the throw line and the report for the GameTests. */
	private void countThrow(ServerPlayer target, @Nullable ThrowInfo info, boolean hit) {
		boolean still = info == null || Dodging.isStill(Math.hypot(target.getX() - info.kidX(), target.getZ() - info.kidZ()));
		tally(target.getUUID()).thrownAt(still, !hit);
		if (info != null) {
			lastThrow = new ThrowReport(target.getPlainTextName(), fightLevel.number(), info.distance(), info.ticks(), info.speed(), still, hit);
			TafelBoem.LOGGER.info("TafelBoem throw: {}", lastThrow.describe());
		}
	}
```

The `hits(...)` helper is not used any more: delete it. At the end of the fight `showRings` stops by itself: add `rings.clear();` to `end()`.

`src/gametest/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/gametest/TerugbomGameTest.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.gametest;

import java.util.List;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTestHelper;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.contents.TranslatableContents;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.Container;
import net.minecraft.world.SimpleContainer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.item.ItemEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.ChestMenu;
import net.minecraft.world.item.BlockItem;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.gametest.v1.GameTest;

import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.config.TafelBoemConfig;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.BombTier;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.FightLevel;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.Pacing;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.item.TerugbomItem;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.registry.ModItems;

/**
 * R1.4's Terugbom: a dodged bomb drops as a glowing item, any kid catches it by walking onto it (at most 3, the
 * slot selected), it poofs after 15 s, and it can't be dropped, placed or put in a chest.
 */
public class TerugbomGameTest {
	@GameTest(maxTicks = 300, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aDodgedBombDropsAsAGlowingTerugbomAndHeTauntsTheKid(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		DodgeGameTest.wallInFrontOf(arena);

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).dodged() >= 1, "wait for his first throw to hit the wall");
			List<ItemEntity> lying = arena.terugbommen();
			helper.assertTrue(lying.size() == 1, "one Terugbom lies there: " + lying.size());
			ItemEntity bomb = lying.getFirst();
			helper.assertTrue(bomb.hasGlowingTag(), "it glows, so it is easy to spot");
			helper.assertTrue(bomb.getItem().getItem() == ModItems.terugbom(FightLevel.MAKKELIJK.bombForThrow(1)), "it is the bomb he threw");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.lyingBombs().isEmpty(), "no lying bomb, no block");
			helper.assertFalse(arena.askedAnything(), "nobody gets a question");
			helper.assertFalse(arena.sessions.isBusy(arena.kid), "nobody is rooted");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.heard(arena.kid, "tafelboem.boss.graaf_fout.dodged"), "he taunts the kid it missed");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void anyKidCatchesItAndItsSlotIsSelected(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		ServerPlayer sibling = arena.addKid(arena.box.middleX() + 8, arena.box.middleZ() + 8);
		// The sibling's selected slot (0) is empty: the bomb goes there. The kid's selected slot (3) is taken, slot 0 is the first empty one.
		arena.kid.getInventory().setItem(3, new ItemStack(Items.DIRT));
		arena.kid.getInventory().setSelectedSlot(3);
		arena.fight.dropTerugbom(sibling.position().add(1, 0, 0), BombTier.GEWONE_TNT);
		arena.fight.dropTerugbom(arena.kid.position().add(-1, 0, 0), BombTier.KNALLETJE);

		helper.runAfterDelay(6, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(sibling.getUUID()).caught() == 1, "the sibling caught theirs: any kid can");
			helper.assertTrue(sibling.getInventory().getItem(0).getItem() == ModItems.terugbom(BombTier.GEWONE_TNT), "in their selected slot");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).caught() == 1, "and the kid theirs");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.kid.getInventory().getItem(0).getItem() == ModItems.terugbom(BombTier.KNALLETJE), "in the first empty slot of the hotbar");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.kid.getInventory().getSelectedSlot() == 0, "which is selected now, so one right-click throws it");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.client.heldSlots.stream().anyMatch(held -> held.player() == arena.kid && held.slot() == 0), "and the client is told");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().isEmpty(), "nothing lies there any more");
			helper.assertTrue(hints(arena, arena.kid) == 1 && hints(arena, sibling) == 1, "each kid is told how to throw, once");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void aFourthIsRefusedAlsoOverSeveralSlotsAndTheOffhand(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		Inventory pocket = arena.kid.getInventory();
		pocket.setItem(5, new ItemStack(ModItems.terugbom(BombTier.KNALLETJE)));
		pocket.setItem(20, new ItemStack(ModItems.terugbom(BombTier.KNALLETJE)));
		pocket.setItem(Inventory.SLOT_OFFHAND, new ItemStack(ModItems.terugbom(BombTier.GEWONE_TNT)));
		arena.fight.dropTerugbom(arena.kid.position().add(1, 0, 0), BombTier.KIPPENBOM);

		helper.runAfterDelay(6, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).caught() == 0, "with 3 in the slots and the offhand a fourth is refused");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().size() == 1, "it stays on the ground");
			pocket.setItem(20, ItemStack.EMPTY);
		});
		helper.runAfterDelay(12, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).caught() == 1, "with room again, it is caught");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().isEmpty(), "and gone from the floor");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aTerugbomNobodyPicksUpPoofsAfterFifteenSeconds(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		// Far from the kid, who stands 5 blocks south of the middle.
		arena.fight.dropTerugbom(new Vec3(arena.box.middleX() - 8, arena.floorY, arena.box.middleZ() - 8), BombTier.KNALLETJE);

		helper.runAfterDelay(Pacing.TERUGBOM_LIFETIME_TICKS - 10, () ->
				helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().size() == 1, "still lying there just before the 15 seconds are up"));
		helper.runAfterDelay(Pacing.TERUGBOM_LIFETIME_TICKS + 10, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().isEmpty(), "poofed after 15 seconds");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).caught() == 0, "nobody caught it");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 60, padding = 48)
	public void itCantBePlacedDroppedOrPutInAChest(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		ItemStack bomb = new ItemStack(ModItems.terugbom(BombTier.KNALLETJE));
		helper.assertFalse(bomb.getItem() instanceof BlockItem, "not a block item: it can't be placed, and never starts a free-play question");
		helper.assertTrue(bomb.getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem, "its own item");

		// Q: the stack leaves the slot and drops; it must be back in the kid's pocket a moment later, and nothing lies there.
		arena.kid.getInventory().setItem(4, ItemStack.EMPTY);
		arena.kid.drop(bomb.copy(), false);
		// A chest: a Terugbom put in a slot of the open menu goes back to the kid's pocket.
		Container chest = new SimpleContainer(27);
		arena.kid.containerMenu = ChestMenu.threeRows(7, arena.kid.getInventory(), chest);
		arena.kid.containerMenu.getSlot(0).set(new ItemStack(ModItems.terugbom(BombTier.GEWONE_TNT)));

		helper.runAfterDelay(4, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().isEmpty(), "the dropped bomb is gone from the floor");
			helper.assertTrue(chest.isEmpty(), "and the chest is empty");
			int carried = 0;
			for (int slot = 0; slot < Inventory.INVENTORY_SIZE; slot++) {
				carried += arena.kid.getInventory().getItem(slot).getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem ? 1 : 0;
			}
			helper.assertTrue(carried == 2, "both are in the kid's pocket: " + carried);
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	/** How many times this kid was told how to throw. */
	private static long hints(ArenaFight arena, ServerPlayer kid) {
		return arena.client.actionBarsTo.stream().filter(bar -> bar.player() == kid && bar.message().getContents() instanceof TranslatableContents says
				&& says.getKey().equals("tafelboem.terugbom.hint")).count();
	}
}
```

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.item;

import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;

import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.BombTier;

/**
 * A bomb Graaf Fout threw and missed, caught by a kid (R1.4). Not a block item on purpose: it can't be placed, so it
 * never starts a free-play question, and a kid can't leave it lying about. Right-clicking it throws it back at him
 * (Task 8 adds that); outside a running fight the game takes it away again.
 */
public class TerugbomItem extends Item {
	private final BombTier tier;

	public TerugbomItem(BombTier tier, Properties properties) {
		super(properties);
		this.tier = tier;
	}

	public BombTier tier() {
		return tier;
	}
}
```

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.boss;

import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;

/**
 * Which kids were told how to throw a Terugbom (R1.4): once per kid per world. A TafelBoem world remembers it in
 * the fights log; a fight without it tells each kid once per fight.
 */
public interface Hints {
	/** Whether this kid was told in this session or in an earlier fight. */
	boolean seen(ServerPlayer kid);

	/** This kid was told just now. */
	void shown(ServerPlayer kid);
}
```

`src/main/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/boss/Terugbommen.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.boss;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.function.BiConsumer;

import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.item.ItemEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.Slot;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.Ammo;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.BombTier;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.Pacing;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.game.BombEffects;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.game.QuizClient;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.item.TerugbomItem;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.registry.ModItems;

/**
 * The Terugbommen of one fight (R1.4): glowing bomb items on the floor that poof after 15 s, and the rules for
 * catching them. The fight hands a bomb over itself instead of leaving it to Minecraft's pick-up, because the cap
 * of 3, the slot and the count in the log are the fight's. Also what a kid's pockets may hold.
 */
final class Terugbommen {
	/** Tags the bomb items this class dropped, so one a kid dropped (Q) is told from one lying here. */
	static final String TAG = "tafelboem_terugbom";

	private static final class Lying {
		final ItemEntity item;
		final BombTier tier;
		int ticksLeft = Pacing.TERUGBOM_LIFETIME_TICKS;

		Lying(ItemEntity item, BombTier tier) {
			this.item = item;
			this.tier = tier;
		}
	}

	private final ServerLevel level;
	private final QuizClient client;
	private final List<Lying> lying = new ArrayList<>();

	Terugbommen(ServerLevel level, QuizClient client) {
		this.level = level;
		this.client = client;
	}

	/** A bomb lies here as an item, with a small poof: glowing, so it is easy to spot. */
	void drop(Vec3 at, BombTier tier) {
		ItemEntity item = new ItemEntity(level, at.x, at.y, at.z, new ItemStack(ModItems.terugbom(tier)));
		item.setNeverPickUp();
		item.setUnlimitedLifetime();
		item.setGlowingTag(true);
		item.setDeltaMovement(Vec3.ZERO);
		// Before it enters the world, so the hook for dropped Terugbommen doesn't take it for a kid's.
		item.addTag(TAG);
		level.addFreshEntity(item);
		BombEffects.poof(level, at);
		lying.add(new Lying(item, tier));
	}

	/** Once a tick: the ones that are over poof, and a kid who walks onto one that they have room for catches it. */
	void tick(List<ServerPlayer> kids, BiConsumer<ServerPlayer, BombTier> caught) {
		for (Iterator<Lying> it = lying.iterator(); it.hasNext(); ) {
			Lying bomb = it.next();
			if (!bomb.item.isAlive()) {
				it.remove();
				continue;
			}
			if (--bomb.ticksLeft <= 0) {
				poof(bomb);
				it.remove();
				continue;
			}
			for (ServerPlayer kid : kids) {
				if (reaches(kid, bomb.item) && Ammo.canCatch(carried(kid)) && give(client, kid, bomb.tier)) {
					bomb.item.discard();
					it.remove();
					caught.accept(kid, bomb.tier);
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	/** The fight is over: everything on the floor poofs. */
	void poofAll() {
		lying.forEach(Terugbommen::poof);
		lying.clear();
	}

	private static void poof(Lying bomb) {
		if (bomb.item.isAlive() && bomb.item.level() instanceof ServerLevel level) {
			BombEffects.poof(level, bomb.item.position());
			bomb.item.discard();
		}
	}

	/** Within Minecraft's own pick-up reach: the kid's box, a block wider and half a block higher. */
	private static boolean reaches(ServerPlayer kid, ItemEntity item) {
		return kid.isAlive() && !kid.isSpectator() && kid.getBoundingBox().inflate(1.0, 0.5, 1.0).intersects(item.getBoundingBox());
	}

	/**
	 * Puts a Terugbom in this kid's pocket where {@link Ammo#slotFor} says, and selects that slot when it is on the
	 * hotbar. The cap is the caller's to check.
	 *
	 * @return false when every slot is taken
	 */
	static boolean give(QuizClient client, ServerPlayer kid, BombTier tier) {
		Inventory inventory = kid.getInventory();
		boolean[] empty = new boolean[Inventory.INVENTORY_SIZE];
		for (int slot = 0; slot < empty.length; slot++) {
			empty[slot] = inventory.getItem(slot).isEmpty();
		}
		int slot = Ammo.slotFor(inventory.getSelectedSlot(), empty);
		if (slot < 0) {
			return false;
		}
		inventory.setItem(slot, new ItemStack(ModItems.terugbom(tier)));
		if (Ammo.isHotbar(slot)) {
			inventory.setSelectedSlot(slot);
			client.selectSlot(kid, slot);
		}
		kid.level().playSound(null, kid.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.ITEM_PICKUP, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.5F, 1.0F);
		return true;
	}

	/** How many Terugbommen this kid carries, over all slots and the offhand. */
	static int carried(ServerPlayer kid) {
		Inventory inventory = kid.getInventory();
		int count = 0;
		for (int slot = 0; slot < Inventory.INVENTORY_SIZE; slot++) {
			count += terugbommenIn(inventory.getItem(slot));
		}
		return count + terugbommenIn(inventory.getItem(Inventory.SLOT_OFFHAND));
	}

	private static int terugbommenIn(ItemStack stack) {
		return stack.getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem ? stack.getCount() : 0;
	}

	/** A Terugbom that ended up in a chest or the crafting grid goes back to the kid's pocket. */
	static void returnFromMenu(QuizClient client, ServerPlayer kid) {
		for (Slot slot : kid.containerMenu.slots) {
			if (slot.container != kid.getInventory() && slot.getItem().getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem terugbom) {
				slot.set(ItemStack.EMPTY);
				give(client, kid, terugbom.tier());
			}
		}
	}
}
```

```java
	public void useHints(@Nullable Hints hints) {

	/** A Terugbom lies here, glowing. A dodge drops one; the GameTests and the client test put one where they need it. */
	public void dropTerugbom(Vec3 at, BombTier tier) {
		terugbommen.drop(at, tier);
	}

	/** A kid caught a Terugbom (the cap and the slot are settled): the tally, and the first time the hint. */
	private void caught(ServerPlayer kid, BombTier tier) {
		tally(kid.getUUID()).catches();
		boolean first = hints == null ? hinted.add(kid.getUUID()) : !hints.seen(kid);
		if (first) {
			sessions.client().actionBar(kid, Component.translatable("tafelboem.terugbom.hint").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD, ChatFormatting.BOLD));
			if (hints != null) {
				hints.shown(kid);
			}
		}
	}
```

```java
	/**
	 * An item entity turned up. A Terugbom a kid dropped (Q, or dragged out of the inventory) can't be dropped: it goes
	 * straight back to them. The ones the fight put on the floor carry its tag and are left alone.
	 */
	public void returnDropped(ItemEntity item) {
		if (!(item.getItem().getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem terugbom) || item.entityTags().contains(Terugbommen.TAG)
				|| !(item.level() instanceof ServerLevel level)) {
			return;
		}
		ServerPlayer kid = item.getOwner() instanceof ServerPlayer owner ? owner : null;
		double nearest = 16;
		for (ServerPlayer player : level.players()) {
			if (kid == null && player.distanceToSqr(item) < nearest) {
				kid = player;
				nearest = player.distanceToSqr(item);
			}
		}
		item.discard();
		if (kid != null) {
			Terugbommen.give(sessions.client(), kid, terugbom.tier());
		}
	}
```

`src/test/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/core/StaggerTest.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class StaggerTest {
	@Test
	void withoutAHitHeNeverSkips() {
		Stagger stagger = new Stagger();
		for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
			assertFalse(stagger.skipsThrow());
		}
	}

	@Test
	void aHitMakesHimSkipHisNextThrow() {
		Stagger stagger = new Stagger();
		stagger.hit();
		assertTrue(stagger.skipsThrow());
		assertFalse(stagger.skipsThrow(), "and only that one");
	}

	@Test
	void twoHitsBeforeAThrowSkipOnlyOneThrow() {
		Stagger stagger = new Stagger();
		stagger.hit();
		stagger.hit();
		assertTrue(stagger.skipsThrow());
		assertFalse(stagger.skipsThrow());
	}

	@Test
	void aStaggerRightAfterASkippedThrowIsUsedUpSoHeSkipsAtMostOneThrowInTwo() {
		Stagger stagger = new Stagger();
		stagger.hit();
		assertTrue(stagger.skipsThrow(), "skipped");
		stagger.hit();
		assertFalse(stagger.skipsThrow(), "the next one he throws, whoever hit him");
		stagger.hit();
		assertTrue(stagger.skipsThrow(), "and the one after that may be skipped again");
	}

	@Test
	void twoKidsHittingHimAllTheTimeStillLetHimThrowEveryOtherTime() {
		Stagger stagger = new Stagger();
		int thrown = 0;
		for (int round = 0; round < 10; round++) {
			stagger.hit();
			stagger.hit();
			if (!stagger.skipsThrow()) {
				thrown++;
			}
		}
		assertTrue(thrown == 5, "5 of 10 throws: " + thrown);
	}
}
```

`src/test/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/core/HomingTest.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class HomingTest {
	/** Graaf Fout's box: 0.6 wide, 1.95 high, standing at the origin on the floor. */
	private static final double HALF_WIDTH = 0.3;
	private static final double HEIGHT = 1.95;
	private static final double SPEED = 1.5;
	private static final double GRAVITY = 0.03;

	@Test
	void steeringKeepsTheSpeedAndTurnsTowardsHim() {
		double[] v = Homing.steer(0, 0, -1.5, 3, 0, -3);

		assertEquals(1.5, Math.sqrt(v[0] * v[0] + v[1] * v[1] + v[2] * v[2]), 1e-9, "the speed stays");
		assertTrue(v[0] > 0, "it bends to the side he is on");
		assertTrue(v[2] < 0, "and still flies forward");
	}

	@Test
	void aBombHeadingStraightForHimKeepsItsCourse() {
		double[] v = Homing.steer(0, 0, -1.5, 0, 0, -4);
		assertEquals(0, v[0], 1e-9);
		assertEquals(-1.5, v[2], 1e-9);
	}

	@Test
	void aBombThatStandsStillOrIsOnTopOfHimIsLeftAlone() {
		assertEquals(0, Homing.steer(0, 0, 0, 3, 0, 0)[0]);
		assertEquals(1.5, Homing.steer(1.5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)[0]);
	}

	@Test
	void theRangeIsFiveBlocksAndSixWhileHeFlies() {
		assertEquals(5, Homing.range(false));
		assertEquals(6, Homing.range(true));
		assertEquals(1, Homing.HIT_MARGIN);
	}

	/**
	 * A kid 12 blocks off throws straight ahead and misses him by {@code aside} blocks; the bomb sags a little
	 * until it is near him, then homes in. Whether it enters his box with its margin, as the spec's aim help
	 * demands: the younger kid hits a standing Graaf Fout at least half the time.
	 */
	private static boolean hits(double aside) {
		double[] p = {aside, 1.4, 12};
		double[] v = {0, 0, -SPEED};
		double margin = Homing.HIT_MARGIN;
		for (int tick = 0; tick < 60; tick++) {
			double[] next = {p[0] + v[0], p[1] + v[1], p[2] + v[2]};
			for (int step = 0; step <= 10; step++) {
				double f = step / 10.0;
				double x = p[0] + (next[0] - p[0]) * f;
				double y = p[1] + (next[1] - p[1]) * f;
				double z = p[2] + (next[2] - p[2]) * f;
				if (Math.abs(x) <= HALF_WIDTH + margin && Math.abs(z) <= HALF_WIDTH + margin && y >= -margin && y <= HEIGHT + margin) {
					return true;
				}
			}
			double[] toHim = {-p[0], HEIGHT / 2 - p[1], -p[2]};
			if (Math.sqrt(toHim[0] * toHim[0] + toHim[1] * toHim[1] + toHim[2] * toHim[2]) <= Homing.RANGE) {
				v = Homing.steer(v[0], v[1], v[2], toHim[0], toHim[1], toHim[2]);
			} else {
				v[1] -= GRAVITY;
			}
			p = next;
			if (p[2] < -8 || p[1] < -3) {
				return false;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	@Test
	void aThrowThatMissesHimByUpToFourBlocksStillHits() {
		for (double aside = 0; aside <= 4.0; aside += 0.5) {
			assertTrue(hits(aside), "missing him by " + aside + " blocks");
		}
	}

	@Test
	void aThrowThatPassesFarOutsideTheRangeMisses() {
		assertFalse(hits(7), "7 blocks beside him is outside the homing range");
	}
}
```

In `ArenaFight` add (imports `net.minecraft.util.Mth`, `net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand`, `nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.registry.ModItems`):

```java
	/** The kid turns to look at this point: a mock player's view is set directly. */
	static void aimAt(ServerPlayer kid, Vec3 point) {
		Vec3 to = point.subtract(kid.getEyePosition());
		kid.setYRot((float) (Mth.atan2(-to.x, to.z) * Mth.RAD_TO_DEG));
		kid.setXRot((float) -(Mth.atan2(to.y, to.horizontalDistance()) * Mth.RAD_TO_DEG));
	}

	/** A Terugbom of this tier in the kid's main hand. */
	static void giveInHand(ServerPlayer kid, BombTier tier) {
		kid.setItemInHand(InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND, new ItemStack(ModItems.terugbom(tier)));
	}

	/** The middle of Graaf Fout, where a thrown-back bomb aims. */
	Vec3 hisMiddle() {
		return fight.boss().position().add(0, fight.boss().getBbHeight() / 2, 0);
	}
```

`src/gametest/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/gametest/ThrowBackGameTest.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.gametest;

import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;

import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTestHelper;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntitySpawnReason;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityTypes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.chicken.Chicken;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.gametest.v1.GameTest;

import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.config.TafelBoemConfig;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.BombTier;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.FightLevel;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.YeetStyle;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.net.QuizS2C;

/**
 * R1.4's throw-back: right-click throws a caught bomb at him, once a second, through kids and chickens; it homes in
 * on him; a hit staggers him and asks the Terug! question; he skips a throw but never two in a row; a right answer
 * is a hit and a throw-back yeet, a wrong one fizzles; and in every state he can't be hit in, it drops back.
 */
public class ThrowBackGameTest {
	@GameTest(maxTicks = 80, padding = 48)
	public void aThrowBackHitsHimStaggersHimAndAsksTheTerugQuestion(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		helper.runAfterDelay(2, () -> {
			ArenaFight.giveInHand(arena.kid, BombTier.GEWONE_TNT);
			ArenaFight.aimAt(arena.kid, arena.hisMiddle());
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND), "the throw flies");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.kid.getItemInHand(InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND).isEmpty(), "and the bomb is used up");
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).backHits() == 1, "wait for it to reach him");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.boss().isStaggered(), "he wobbles");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.asked(arena.kid, BombTier.GEWONE_TNT), "the thrower is asked a question on the caught bomb's tier");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.sessions.isBusy(arena.kid), "rooted, as after a hit");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).thrownBack() == 1, "counted as thrown back");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 80, padding = 48)
	public void aKidThrowsBackAtMostOnceASecond(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		// 9 blocks from him, throwing straight east: out of his homing range, so nothing hits him and the kid is never asked.
		arena.kid.teleportTo(arena.box.middleX(), arena.floorY, arena.box.middleZ() + 9);
		ArenaFight.giveInHand(arena.kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
		ArenaFight.aimAt(arena.kid, arena.kid.getEyePosition().add(30, 0, 0));
		helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND), "the first throw flies");

		ArenaFight.giveInHand(arena.kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
		helper.assertFalse(arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND), "a second in the same tick doesn't");
		helper.runAfterDelay(19, () -> helper.assertFalse(arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND), "nor one tick short of a second"));
		helper.runAfterDelay(21, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND), "a second later it does");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 80, padding = 48)
	public void itPassesThroughAKidAndAChickenOnTheWay(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		ServerPlayer sibling = arena.addKid(arena.box.middleX(), arena.box.middleZ() + 2.5);
		Chicken chicken = EntityTypes.CHICKEN.create(arena.level, EntitySpawnReason.TRIGGERED);
		chicken.setPos(arena.box.middleX(), arena.floorY, arena.box.middleZ() + 3.5);
		chicken.setNoAi(true);
		arena.level.addFreshEntity(chicken);
		helper.runAfterDelay(2, () -> {
			ArenaFight.giveInHand(arena.kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
			ArenaFight.aimAt(arena.kid, arena.hisMiddle());
			arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).backHits() == 1, "wait for it to reach him, past the sibling and the chicken");
			helper.assertFalse(arena.asked(sibling, BombTier.KNALLETJE), "the sibling was not hit");
			helper.assertTrue(chicken.isAlive(), "and the chicken is fine");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 80, padding = 48)
	public void homingHitsAStandingGraafFoutFromFourBlocksOffAim(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		// 9 blocks from him, aiming 4 blocks beside him: the straight flight misses by 4, the homing range is 5.
		arena.kid.teleportTo(arena.box.middleX(), arena.floorY, arena.box.middleZ() + 9);
		helper.runAfterDelay(2, () -> {
			ArenaFight.giveInHand(arena.kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
			ArenaFight.aimAt(arena.kid, arena.hisMiddle().add(4, 0, 0));
			arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).backHits() == 1, "it bent in on him and hit");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 120, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aThrowBackThatMissesPoofsAndAsksNothing(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		helper.runAfterDelay(2, () -> {
			ArenaFight.giveInHand(arena.kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
			// Straight east, level, far from him: nothing to home in on.
			ArenaFight.aimAt(arena.kid, arena.kid.getEyePosition().add(30, 0, 0));
			arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
		});

		helper.runAfterDelay(58, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.bombsInTheAir().isEmpty(), "it poofed: nothing flies any more");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).backHits() == 0, "no hit");
			helper.assertFalse(arena.askedAnything(), "no question");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().isEmpty(), "and it doesn't drop back: a miss is gone");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 300, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aHitSkipsHisNextThrowButNotTwoInARow(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		Set<Integer> answered = new HashSet<>();
		long[] firstBombAt = {-1};
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			// Every question is answered wrong at once, so the kid is free again and nothing yeets him.
			for (QuizS2C question : arena.questionsTo(arena.kid)) {
				if (answered.add(question.sessionId())) {
					arena.answerWrong(arena.kid, question);
				}
			}
			if (firstBombAt[0] < 0 && !arena.bombsInTheAir().isEmpty()) {
				firstBombAt[0] = helper.getTick();
			}
		});
		// Two hits, the second just before the throw he would then make: it must not skip a second one.
		for (int tick : new int[] {2, 90}) {
			helper.runAfterDelay(tick, () -> {
				ArenaFight.giveInHand(arena.kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
				ArenaFight.aimAt(arena.kid, arena.hisMiddle());
				arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
			});
		}

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(firstBombAt[0] >= 0, "wait for his first throw");
			// Without the stagger it comes at tick 60; the hit skips it, the pause after it is 50 ticks.
			helper.assertTrue(firstBombAt[0] >= 100, "the first throw was skipped: it came at tick " + firstBombAt[0]);
			helper.assertTrue(firstBombAt[0] <= 135, "but not a second one in a row: it came at tick " + firstBombAt[0]);
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aRightTerugAnswerIsAHitAndAThrowBackYeet(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());

			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.lastYeet().factor() >= 1.25, "a throw-back yeet, x1.25 at least: " + arena.fight.lastYeet().describe());
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).terugRight() == 1, "counted as a right Terug! answer");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.creditedKid().equals(arena.kid.getUUID()), "credited to the kid who threw it");

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 100, padding = 48)
	public void aThrowBackBouncesOffAStaggeredOrBeatenHimAndAKidWhoIsAnswering(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		ServerPlayer sibling = arena.addKid(arena.box.middleX() + 6, arena.box.middleZ() + 6);
		int[] dropped = {0};
		// 1. Staggered: the first throw-back hits, the sibling's right after it bounces.
		helper.runAfterDelay(2, () -> {
			arena.fight.backReached(arena.kid.getUUID(), BombTier.KNALLETJE);
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.boss().isStaggered(), "the first hit staggers him");
			arena.fight.backReached(sibling.getUUID(), BombTier.KNALLETJE);
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().size() == 1, "the sibling's bounced off and dropped below him");
			helper.assertFalse(arena.asked(sibling, BombTier.KNALLETJE), "and asks nothing");
			dropped[0] = arena.terugbommen().size();
		});
		// 2. A kid who is answering: their throw-back, in the air when the bomb landed on them, bounces.
		helper.runAfterDelay(4, () -> {
			arena.sessions.start(sibling, BombTier.GEWONE_TNT, sibling.blockPosition().east(), "test", null);
			arena.fight.backReached(sibling.getUUID(), BombTier.GEWONE_TNT);
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().size() == dropped[0] + 1, "dropped back as well");
		});
		// 3. His finale.
		helper.runAfterDelay(6, () -> {
			arena.fight.boss().finalYeet();
			arena.fight.backReached(arena.kid.getUUID(), BombTier.KIPPENBOM);
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().size() == dropped[0] + 2, "in his finale it bounces too");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The rules and the constants**

`src/main/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/core/Stagger.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core;

/**
 * The throws a throw-back costs Graaf Fout (R1.4): a hit makes him skip his next throw, but never two
 * throws in a row, so two kids can't keep him from throwing at all.
 */
public final class Stagger {
	private boolean pending;
	private boolean skippedLast;

	/** A throw-back hit him. */
	public void hit() {
		pending = true;
	}

	/**
	 * He is about to throw.
	 *
	 * @return whether he skips this throw instead; a stagger that comes right after a skipped throw is used up
	 */
	public boolean skipsThrow() {
		boolean skip = pending && !skippedLast;
		pending = false;
		skippedLast = skip;
		return skip;
	}
}
```

`src/main/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/core/Homing.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core;

/**
 * The aim help of a Terugbom thrown back (R1.4): near Graaf Fout it bends towards his middle each tick,
 * and its hit box around him is 1 block bigger than his own. The younger kid hits a standing Graaf Fout
 * at least half the time.
 */
public final class Homing {
	/** Within this many blocks of his middle the bomb homes in on him. */
	public static final double RANGE = 5;
	/** While he flies from a yeet. */
	public static final double RANGE_WHILE_FLYING = 6;
	/** His hit box grows by this many blocks on every side. */
	public static final double HIT_MARGIN = 1;
	/** How much of each tick's direction is the direction to him: 0 flies straight, 1 steers at once. */
	public static final double BEND = 0.5;

	private Homing() {
	}

	public static double range(boolean heFlies) {
		return heFlies ? RANGE_WHILE_FLYING : RANGE;
	}

	/**
	 * The bomb's velocity for the next tick: its direction turned towards him by {@link #BEND}, its speed as it was.
	 *
	 * @param vx the bomb's velocity
	 * @param dx from the bomb to his middle
	 */
	public static double[] steer(double vx, double vy, double vz, double dx, double dy, double dz) {
		double speed = Math.sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy + vz * vz);
		double wanted = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
		if (speed == 0 || wanted == 0) {
			return new double[] {vx, vy, vz};
		}
		double x = vx / speed * (1 - BEND) + dx / wanted * BEND;
		double y = vy / speed * (1 - BEND) + dy / wanted * BEND;
		double z = vz / speed * (1 - BEND) + dz / wanted * BEND;
		double length = Math.sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z);
		if (length == 0) {
			return new double[] {vx, vy, vz};
		}
		return new double[] {x / length * speed, y / length * speed, z / length * speed};
	}
}
```

```java
	/** A throw-back hit him: he wobbles for a moment, arms up, and says "ouch" in his own way. */
	public void stagger(int ticks) {
		staggerTicks = ticks;
		armsUp(true);
		playSound(SoundEvents.EVOKER_HURT, 1.2F, 1.5F);
	}

	public boolean isStaggered() {
		return staggerTicks > 0;
	}

	/** Flying from a yeet (not the finale). */

		return yeet != null && !finale;
	}

		level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CLOUD, getX(), getY() + 2.1, getZ(), 2, 0.3, 0.1, 0.3, 0.02);
		if (--staggerTicks == 0) {
			armsUp(false);
```

```java
	/** Thrown back at the boss by a kid who caught it (R1.4): flies like a snowball, homes in on him, hits nothing else. */
	static ThrownBomb thrownBack(ServerLevel level, ServerPlayer kid, BombTier tier, BossFight fight) {
		ThrownBomb bomb = new ThrownBomb(ModEntities.THROWN_BOMB, level);
		bomb.setItem(new ItemStack(ModBlocks.bombItem(tier)));
		bomb.setOwner(kid);
		bomb.tier = tier;
		bomb.fight = fight;
		bomb.thrower = kid.getUUID();
		return bomb;
	}

	/** It passes through kids, chickens and every other mob; only blocks stop it, and Graaf Fout is found in {@link #flyBack}. */
	@Override
	protected boolean canHitEntity(Entity entity) {
		return thrower == null && super.canHitEntity(entity);
	}
```

and the flight:

```java
	/**
	 * One tick of a bomb thrown back: did this tick's path pass through his box (his own, grown by a block), else bend
	 * towards his middle when he is within reach.
	 */
	private void flyBack(ServerLevel level) {
		GraafFout boss = fight.boss();
		if (++backTicks > MAX_BACK_TICKS || !boss.isAlive() || fight.isOver()) {
			missed(level);
			return;
		}
		Vec3 from = position();
		Vec3 velocity = getDeltaMovement();
		AABB box = boss.getBoundingBox().inflate(Homing.HIT_MARGIN);
		if (box.contains(from) || box.clip(from, from.add(velocity)).isPresent()) {
			done = true;
			discard();
			fight.backReached(thrower, tier);
			return;
		}
		Vec3 toHim = boss.position().add(0, boss.getBbHeight() / 2, 0).subtract(from);
		if (toHim.length() <= Homing.range(boss.isFlying())) {
			double[] steered = Homing.steer(velocity.x, velocity.y, velocity.z, toHim.x, toHim.y, toHim.z);
			setDeltaMovement(steered[0], steered[1], steered[2]);
			// Near him it flies straight at him instead of sagging.
			setNoGravity(true);
		}
	}

	/** A miss (a block, or too long in the air) just poofs. */
	private void missed(ServerLevel level) {
		done = true;
		discard();
		BombEffects.poof(level, position());
	}
```
