New methods:

```java
	/**
	 * A kid right-clicked a Terugbom: it flies at him like a snowball, at most once a second (R1.4). Not for a kid who is
	 * answering, not in a fight that is decided, and not with dodging off.
	 *
	 * @return whether it flew
	 */
	public boolean throwBack(ServerPlayer kid, InteractionHand hand) {
		ItemStack stack = kid.getItemInHand(hand);
		if (!(stack.getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem terugbom) || !dodging() || decided() || !kid.isAlive() || sessions.isBusy(kid)
				|| !participants.players().contains(kid)) {
			return false;
		}
		long now = level.getGameTime();
		if (now - lastBack.getOrDefault(kid.getUUID(), now - Pacing.BACK_COOLDOWN_TICKS) < Pacing.BACK_COOLDOWN_TICKS) {
			return false;
		}
		lastBack.put(kid.getUUID(), now);
		// Used up, in creative too: a Terugbom is ammunition, not an item to keep.
		kid.setItemInHand(hand, ItemStack.EMPTY);
		ThrownBomb bomb = ThrownBomb.thrownBack(level, kid, terugbom.tier(), this);
		bomb.setPos(kid.getX(), kid.getEyeY() - 0.1, kid.getZ());
		bomb.shootFromRotation(kid, kid.getXRot(), kid.getYRot(), 0.0F, BACK_SPEED, 0.0F);
		level.addFreshEntity(bomb);
		level.playSound(null, kid.blockPosition(), SoundEvents.WITCH_THROW, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.6F, 1.4F);
		tally(kid.getUUID()).throwBack();
		return true;
	}

	/**
	 * A Terugbom a kid threw back reached him. While he hovers it staggers him and the kid gets the Terug! question;
	 * in every state he can't be hit in it bounces off and drops back as a Terugbom. The bomb in the air calls this;
	 * the GameTests and the client test call it on cue.
	 */
	public void backReached(UUID throwerId, BombTier tier) {
		ServerPlayer thrower = level.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayer(throwerId);
		if (bounces(thrower)) {
			dropBack(tier);
			return;
		}
		tally(throwerId).backHit();
		boss.stagger(Pacing.STAGGER_TICKS);
		stagger.hit();
		if (ask(thrower, tier, boss.blockPosition(), QuestionKind.TERUG) == null) {
			BombEffects.fizzle(level, boss.blockPosition());
		}
	}

	/**
	 * Why a throw-back can't hit him now: he is beaten, staggered, flying or landing, or the kid who threw it is now
	 * answering something else, has left or has died.
	 */
	private boolean bounces(@Nullable ServerPlayer thrower) {
		return thrower == null || !thrower.isAlive() || sessions.isBusy(thrower) || !participants.players().contains(thrower)
				|| decided() || boss.isStaggered() || boss.isFinale() || boss.isReacting();
	}

	/** It bounces off him and drops below him as a Terugbom anyone can catch. */
	private void dropBack(BombTier tier) {
		terugbommen.drop(floorBelow(boss.position()), tier);
	}

	/** The arena floor straight below this spot: kept inside the arena, and never over the void. */
	private Vec3 floorBelow(Vec3 at) {
		double x = at.x;
		double z = at.z;
		if (arena != null) {
			x = Math.max(arena.minX() + 1, Math.min(arena.maxX(), x));
			z = Math.max(arena.minZ() + 1, Math.min(arena.maxZ(), z));
		}
		int ground = level.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING, Mth.floor(x), Mth.floor(z));
		if (arena != null && ground <= arena.minY()) {
			x = arena.middleX();
			z = arena.middleZ();
			ground = level.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING, Mth.floor(x), Mth.floor(z));
		}
		return new Vec3(x, ground, z);
	}
```

Replace `onCorrect` by the version that tells the kinds apart, and `onKickHit` where it picks the power and direction; `onMiss` counts a typed wrong Terug! answer and leaves his rhythm alone:

```java
	@Override
	public void onCorrect(ServerPlayer player, QuizSession session) {
		QuestionKind kind = open.remove(player.getUUID());
		if (kind == null) {
			kind = QuestionKind.BOMB;
		}
		FightTally tally = tally(player.getUUID());
		tally.record(Outcome.CORRECT);
		Kick kick = new Kick(player.getUUID(), session.fact(), session.tier(), session.answerMillis(), session.usualMillis(), session.wasFast(),
				tally.streak(), kind.yeetKind());
		if (kind != QuestionKind.BOMB) {
			// The Terug! question: it blows up on him, the same as a kick that has just hit him.
			tally.terug(true);
			sessions.client().track(player, boss, KICK_CAM_MAX_TICKS, true);
			onKickHit(boss.position(), session.tier(), kick);
			return;
		}
		BlockPos pos = session.bombPos();
		if (level.getBlockState(pos).getBlock() instanceof BombBlock) {
			level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), Block.UPDATE_ALL);
		}
		ThrownBomb thrown = ThrownBomb.kicked(level, player, session.tier(), this, boss, kick);
		thrown.setPos(Vec3.atCenterOf(pos));
		level.addFreshEntity(thrown);

		// The simple kick: a vanilla swing everyone sees, a whoosh, and a camera bump on the kid's screen.
		player.swing(InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND, true);
		level.playSound(null, pos, SoundEvents.PLAYER_ATTACK_SWEEP, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.2F, 0.8F);
		sessions.client().track(player, boss, KICK_CAM_MAX_TICKS, true);

		if (!decided()) {
			phase = Phase.KICK_IN_FLIGHT;
			phaseTicks = FLIGHT_TIMEOUT;
		}
	}
```

Replace `onMiss` (Task 5 gave it `open.remove` and the guard; now it also counts a typed wrong Terug! answer and leaves his rhythm alone):

```java
	@Override
	public void onMiss(ServerPlayer player, QuizSession session, Outcome outcome) {
		QuestionKind kind = open.remove(player.getUUID());
		tally(player.getUUID()).record(outcome);
		if (kind != null && kind != QuestionKind.BOMB && outcome == Outcome.WRONG) {
			tally(player.getUUID()).terug(false);
		}
		// Only a typed wrong answer: "?" must stay better than guessing.
		if (outcome == Outcome.WRONG && config.wrongAnswerDamage) {
			BombEffects.hurt(player, fightLevel.wrongAnswerDamage());
		}
		int a = session.fact().a();
		int b = session.fact().b();
		Component taunt = switch (outcome) {
			case WRONG -> Component.translatable("tafelboem.boss.graaf_fout.taunt.wrong", a, b, a * b);
			case CHICKEN_OUT -> Component.translatable("tafelboem.boss.graaf_fout.taunt.chicken_out");
			default -> Component.translatable("tafelboem.boss.graaf_fout.taunt.dont_know", a, b);
		};
		participants.say(taunt);
		boss.playSound(SoundEvents.EVOKER_CELEBRATE, 1.0F, 1.2F);
		// Only a question about a bomb he threw restarts his pause; a Terug! answer leaves his rhythm alone, and so does
		// a sibling's miss while he flies or lands.
		if ((kind == null || kind == QuestionKind.BOMB) && phase != Phase.REACTING) {
			cooldown(AFTER_MISS_COOLDOWN);
		}
	}
```

Replace `onKickHit` (Task 5 gave it the kick; now the kick's kind decides the power and the direction):

```java
	void onKickHit(Vec3 where, BombTier tier, @Nullable Kick kick) {
		if (decided()) {
			return;
		}
		YeetKind kind = kick == null ? YeetKind.KICK : kick.kind();
		// The bigger the bomb, the bigger the bang and the further he flies.
		boom(new Vec3(boss.getX(), boss.getY() + 1, boss.getZ()), tier);
		if (tier == BombTier.KIPPENBOM && config.kippenbomChickens) {
			if (config.countingKippenbom && kick != null) {
				sessions.countChickens(level, boss.position(), kick.fact(), "fight");
			} else {
				BombEffects.flyingChickens(level, boss.position());
				sessions.collectSpectacles(level, boss.blockPosition());
			}
		}
		if (hit()) {
			return;
		}

		ServerPlayer kicker = kick == null ? null : level.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayer(kick.kicker());
		Vec3 kickedFrom = kicker != null ? kicker.position() : where;
		yeetKick = kick;
		yeetFactor = kick == null ? 1.0 : kick.factor(config.yeetBoost);
		double power = YeetPower.power(kind, 0.9 + level.getRandom().nextDouble() * 0.5, tier.yeetPower(), yeetFactor);
		reactionCooldown = COOLDOWN_TICKS;
		// A kicked bomb sends him away from where it hit; a throw-back from the kid who threw it.
		boss.yeet(kind == YeetKind.KICK ? where : kickedFrom, power, reactions.nextFlight(arena != null, config.yeetCatalogue), kickedFrom, traps());
		phase = Phase.REACTING;
		phaseTicks = REACTION_MIN_TICKS;
	}
```

```java
	@GameTest(maxTicks = 400, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void aThrowBackYeetHasItsOwnRecordAndIsMarkedOnTheYeetLine(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		Map<YeetKind, Integer> bests = new EnumMap<>(YeetKind.class);
		// A huge kick record that no throw-back may touch, and a small throw-back best to beat.
		bests.put(YeetKind.KICK, 999);
		bests.put(YeetKind.BACK, 1);
		List<YeetKind> beaten = new ArrayList<>();
		arena.fight.useYeetRecords(new YeetRecords() {
			@Override
			public int best(ServerPlayer kid, YeetKind kind) {
				return bests.getOrDefault(kind, 0);
			}

			@Override
			public void beaten(ServerPlayer kid, YeetKind kind, int blocks) {
				bests.put(kind, blocks);
				beaten.add(kind);
			}
		});
		Set<Integer> answered = new HashSet<>();
		helper.runAfterDelay(2, () -> {
			ArenaFight.giveInHand(arena.kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
			ArenaFight.aimAt(arena.kid, arena.hisMiddle());
			arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
		});
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			for (QuizS2C question : arena.questionsTo(arena.kid)) {
				if (arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).backHits() == 1 && answered.add(question.sessionId())) {
					arena.answerRight(arena.kid, question);
				}
			}
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			YeetReport report = arena.fight.lastYeet();
			helper.assertTrue(report != null, "wait for his flight to end");
			helper.assertTrue(report.kind() == YeetKind.BACK, "the yeet line marks a throw-back: " + report.describe());
			helper.assertTrue(report.describe().contains(", back"), "in its text: " + report.describe());
			helper.assertTrue(bests.get(YeetKind.KICK) == 999 && !beaten.contains(YeetKind.KICK), "a throw-back never touches the kick record");
			if (report.high()) {
				// A helicopter says how high he flew, and a height is no record.
				helper.assertFalse(report.record(), "a height is never a record");
			} else {
				helper.assertTrue(report.record() && beaten.contains(YeetKind.BACK), "it beat the throw-back best of 1 block: " + report.describe());
				helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).farthestBack() == report.blocks(), "the fights log gets it as the farthest throw-back");
				helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).farthestYeet() == 0, "and the farthest kick stays 0");
				helper.assertTrue(arena.client.actionBarsTo.stream().anyMatch(bar -> bar.player() == arena.kid
						&& bar.message().getContents() instanceof TranslatableContents says && says.getKey().equals("tafelboem.message.yeet_record_back")),
						"the kid hears about their throw-back record");
			}
			arena.end();
		});
	}
```

```java
public interface YeetRecords {
	/** The kid's farthest yeet of this kind so far in blocks; 0 when there is none on file. */
	int best(ServerPlayer kid, YeetKind kind);

	/** A new farthest yeet of this kind, kept for the rest of the session; after that the fights log has it. */
	void beaten(ServerPlayer kid, YeetKind kind, int blocks);
}
```

`YeetMeter`: import `nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.YeetKind`; replace `landed` and `forKicker`, add the two key methods:

```java
	void landed(Yeet yeet, boolean flewOut, @Nullable Kick kick, String landing, boolean signature, double factor,
			@Nullable String trap) {
		ServerLevel level = (ServerLevel) boss.level();
		ServerPlayer kicker = kick == null ? null : level.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayer(kick.kicker());
		YeetKind kind = kick == null ? YeetKind.KICK : kick.kind();
		int blocks = yeet.blocks();
		int previousBest = 0;
		boolean record = false;
		if (kicker != null && records != null && !yeet.measuresHeight()) {
			previousBest = records.best(kicker, kind);
			if (blocks > previousBest) {
				records.beaten(kicker, kind, blocks);
				// The first yeet of a kind on file only sets the best: the first one after the update is no record.
				record = previousBest > 0 && config.yeetRecords;
			}
		}

		if (flewOut || blocks >= 1) {
			// A helicopter says how high, even when it drops him out of the arena: a height is no distance.
			String key = yeet.measuresHeight() ? "tafelboem.message.yeet_height" : flewOut ? "tafelboem.message.yeet_out" : distanceKey(kind);
			Component plain = Component.translatable(key, boss.getDisplayName(), blocks).withStyle(ChatFormatting.AQUA, ChatFormatting.BOLD);
			for (ServerPlayer player : flewOut ? participants.players() : participants.nearBoss()) {
				client.actionBar(player, player == kicker ? forKicker(plain, kick, record, blocks) : plain);
			}
		}
		if (record) {
			client.sound(kicker, SoundEvents.UI_TOAST_CHALLENGE_COMPLETE);
		}
		if (kick != null) {
			String name = kicker != null ? kicker.getPlainTextName() : kick.kicker().toString();
			lastReport = new YeetReport(name, kick.tier(), kick.fact(), kick.millis(), kick.usual(), kick.fast(), kick.streak(), factor,
					yeet.style(), landing, signature, blocks, yeet.measuresHeight(), flewOut, record, previousBest, trap, kind);
			TafelBoem.LOGGER.info("TafelBoem yeet: {}", lastReport.describe());
		}
	}

	private static String distanceKey(YeetKind kind) {
		return switch (kind) {
			case KICK -> "tafelboem.message.yeet_distance";
			case BACK -> "tafelboem.message.yeet_back";
			case SUPER -> "tafelboem.message.yeet_super";
		};
	}

	private static String recordKey(YeetKind kind) {
		return switch (kind) {
			case KICK -> "tafelboem.message.yeet_record";
			case BACK -> "tafelboem.message.yeet_record_back";
			case SUPER -> "tafelboem.message.yeet_record_super";
		};
	}

	/** The kicker's own meter: a new record of this kind, or why he flew further. */
	private Component forKicker(Component plain, Kick kick, boolean record, int blocks) {
		if (record) {
			return Component.translatable(recordKey(kick.kind()), boss.getDisplayName(), blocks).withStyle(ChatFormatting.GOLD, ChatFormatting.BOLD);
		}
		Component why = why(kick);
		return why == null ? plain : plain.copy().append(" ").append(why);
	}
```

`TafelBoemWorld`: import `nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.YeetKind`; replace the `yeetRecords` field (the map `bestYeets` stays, now keyed by name and kind):

```java
	/** Each kid's farthest yeet of each kind, from the fights log, kept up to date during the session. */
	private final YeetRecords yeetRecords = new YeetRecords() {
		@Override
		public int best(ServerPlayer kid, YeetKind kind) {
			return bestYeets.computeIfAbsent(key(kid, kind), key -> fightLog.read(kid.getPlainTextName()).stream()
					.mapToInt(fight -> switch (kind) {
						case KICK -> fight.farthestYeet();
						case BACK -> fight.farthestBack();
						case SUPER -> fight.farthestSuper();
					}).max().orElse(0));
		}

		@Override
		public void beaten(ServerPlayer kid, YeetKind kind, int blocks) {
			bestYeets.put(key(kid, kind), Math.max(best(kid, kind), blocks));
		}

		private String key(ServerPlayer kid, YeetKind kind) {
			return kid.getPlainTextName() + ":" + kind.id();
		}
	};
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing GameTests**

`src/gametest/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/gametest/JuggleGameTest.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.gametest;

import java.util.List;

import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTestHelper;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.chicken.Chicken;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.Heightmap;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.gametest.v1.GameTest;

import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.boss.BossFight;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.boss.GraafFout;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.config.TafelBoemConfig;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.AnswerRecord;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.ArenaBox;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.BombTier;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.FightLevel;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.Pacing;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.YeetKind;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.YeetStyle;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.world.ArenaRun;

/**
 * R1.4's juggle: a throw-back that hits him mid-yeet freezes him in the air with the Terug! question open, however
 * long the kid thinks. Right: a hit and a super yeet. Wrong, a timeout or a disconnect: he falls the normal way.
 * One juggle per yeet, never on a super yeet, and the first of two kids' throw-backs counts.
 */
public class JuggleGameTest {
	@GameTest(maxTicks = 100, padding = 48)
	public void aThrowBackMidYeetFreezesHimAndAsksTheJuggleQuestion(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		startJuggle(helper, arena, BombTier.GEWONE_TNT);

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.boss().isFrozen(), "wait for the hit: he hangs in the air");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.asked(arena.kid, BombTier.GEWONE_TNT) && arena.sessions.isBusy(arena.kid), "the thrower is asked, rooted");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).juggles() == 1 && arena.fight.tally(arena.kid.getUUID()).backHits() == 1,
					"counted as a juggle and a hit that reached him");
			helper.assertFalse(arena.fight.boss().isStaggered(), "flying, he doesn't stagger");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 500, padding = 48)
	@Fight
	public void heStaysFrozenPast300TicksWhileTheQuestionIsOpen(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		startJuggle(helper, arena, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
		long[] frozenAt = {-1};
		Vec3[] hangingAt = {null};
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			if (frozenAt[0] < 0 && arena.fight.boss().isFrozen()) {
				frozenAt[0] = helper.getTick();
				hangingAt[0] = arena.fight.boss().position();
			}
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(frozenAt[0] >= 0, "wait for the freeze");
			helper.assertTrue(helper.getTick() - frozenAt[0] >= Pacing.STUCK_TICKS + 20, "wait past the stall net's 300 ticks");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.boss().isFrozen(), "he still hangs there");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.fight.boss().position().distanceTo(hangingAt[0]) < 0.05, "in the same place: the stall net didn't bring him back");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.sessions.isBusy(arena.kid), "the question is still open, and no timer ran out");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.bombsInTheAir().isEmpty(), "he threw nothing: no fight timer runs");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 600, padding = TestWorld.PADDING)
	@Fight
	public void aRightJuggleAnswerIsAHitAndASuperYeetThatLandsInsideTheArenaOnLevel3(GameTestHelper helper) {
		TestWorld test = new TestWorld(helper).running();
		ServerPlayer kid = test.kidInTheHub();
		test.world.enterDoor(kid, 3);
		int[] step = {0};
		int[] waited = {0};
		int[] startHits = {0};
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			BossFight fight = test.world.run(FightLevel.MOEILIJK).map(ArenaRun::fight).orElse(null);
			if (fight == null) {
				return;
			}
			ArenaBox box = fight.arena();
			GraafFout boss = fight.boss();
			if (step[0] == 0) {
				// In the middle, flying up; the kid north of him, so a super yeet takes him south, across the wide part of the floor.
				int ground = test.level.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING, (int) box.middleX(), (int) box.middleZ());
				boss.teleportTo(box.middleX(), ground + 1.5, box.middleZ());
				kid.teleportTo(box.middleX(), ground, box.middleZ() - 6);
				Vec3 south = new Vec3(box.middleX(), ground, box.middleZ() + 3);
				boss.yeet(south, 1.0, YeetStyle.HELICOPTER, south);
				startHits[0] = fight.hitsRemaining();
				step[0] = 1;
			} else if (step[0] == 1 && ++waited[0] >= 12) {
				ArenaFight.giveInHand(kid, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
				ArenaFight.aimAt(kid, boss.position().add(0, boss.getBbHeight() / 2, 0));
				fight.throwBack(kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
				step[0] = 2;
			}
		});

		// TestWorld answers every question right at once: the juggle question too.
		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			BossFight fight = test.world.run(FightLevel.MOEILIJK).map(ArenaRun::fight).orElse(null);
			helper.assertTrue(fight != null && fight.lastYeet() != null, "wait for the super yeet to land");
			helper.assertTrue(fight.lastYeet().kind() == YeetKind.SUPER, "it was a super yeet: " + fight.lastYeet().describe());
			helper.assertTrue(fight.hitsRemaining() == startHits[0] - 1, "and one hit: " + fight.hitsRemaining() + " left of " + startHits[0]);
			GraafFout boss = fight.boss();
			ArenaBox box = fight.arena();
			double fromMiddle = Math.hypot(boss.getX() - box.middleX(), boss.getZ() - box.middleZ());
			helper.assertTrue(fromMiddle < 22.0, "he landed inside the arena, " + fromMiddle + " blocks from its middle");
			List<AnswerRecord> answers = test.sessions.learners().log().read(kid.getPlainTextName());
			helper.assertTrue(answers.stream().anyMatch(answer -> answer.context().equals("juggle") && answer.correct()),
					"the answer log says the sum was a juggle's: " + answers);
			test.close();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 80, padding = 48)
	public void thereIsNoSecondJuggleInOneFlightAndASecondKidsThrowBackDropsBack(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		ServerPlayer sibling = arena.addKid(arena.box.middleX() + 6, arena.box.middleZ() + 6);
		startJuggle(helper, arena, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
		int[] stage = {0};
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			if (stage[0] == 0 && arena.fight.boss().isFrozen()) {
				stage[0] = 1;
				// While he hangs: the sibling's throw-back drops back, it is the first one's juggle that counts.
				arena.fight.backReached(sibling.getUUID(), BombTier.GEWONE_TNT);
				helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().size() == 1, "the sibling's bounced off: " + arena.terugbommen().size());
				helper.assertFalse(arena.asked(sibling, BombTier.GEWONE_TNT), "and asked nothing");
				arena.answerWrong(arena.kid, arena.questionsTo(arena.kid).getFirst());
			} else if (stage[0] == 1 && !arena.fight.boss().isFrozen()) {
				stage[0] = 2;
				// He flies on after the wrong answer: a second juggle in the same flight is not allowed.
				arena.fight.backReached(sibling.getUUID(), BombTier.KNALLETJE);
				helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().size() == 2, "a second throw-back in the same flight drops back too");
				helper.assertFalse(arena.fight.boss().isFrozen(), "he is not frozen again");
			}
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(stage[0] == 2, "wait for both throw-backs");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	public void aSuperYeetCantBeJuggled(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		startJuggle(helper, arena, BombTier.KNALLETJE);
		int[] stage = {0};
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {

				stage[0] = 1;
				arena.answerRight(arena.kid, arena.questionsTo(arena.kid).getFirst());
			} else if (stage[0] == 1 && !arena.fight.boss().isFrozen() && arena.fight.boss().isFlying()) {
				stage[0] = 2;
				// He flies from the super yeet now: a throw-back that reaches him drops back.
				arena.fight.backReached(arena.kid.getUUID(), BombTier.KNALLETJE);
				helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().size() == 1, "it dropped back");
				helper.assertFalse(arena.fight.boss().isFrozen(), "he stays in his super yeet");
			}
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {

			arena.end();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 120, padding = 48)
	public void aKippenbomJugglesChickensComeDownAtTheFloor(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		startJuggle(helper, arena, BombTier.KIPPENBOM);
		double[] hangingY = {0};
		boolean[] answered = {false};
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			if (!answered[0] && arena.fight.boss().isFrozen()) {
				answered[0] = true;
				hangingY[0] = arena.fight.boss().getY();
				arena.answerRight(arena.kid, arena.questionsTo(arena.kid).getFirst());
			}
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(answered[0], "wait for the freeze and the right answer");
			helper.assertTrue(hangingY[0] >= arena.floorY + 3, "he hung well above the floor: " + hangingY[0]);
			List<Chicken> chickens = arena.level.getEntitiesOfClass(Chicken.class,
					new AABB(arena.box.minX(), arena.box.minY(), arena.box.minZ(), arena.box.maxX() + 1, arena.box.maxY() + 30, arena.box.maxZ() + 1));
			helper.assertFalse(chickens.isEmpty(), "chickens came out");
			helper.assertTrue(chickens.stream().allMatch(chicken -> chicken.getY() < arena.floorY + 2.6), "all of them at the arena floor, not in the air");
			arena.end();
		});
	}

	/** A helicopter yeet on cue (he hovers up over the kids for a while), and a throw-back of this tier into him in mid-air. */
	private static void startJuggle(GameTestHelper helper, ArenaFight arena, BombTier tier) {
		helper.runAfterDelay(2, () -> arena.fight.boss().yeet(arena.kid.position(), 1.0, YeetStyle.HELICOPTER, arena.kid.position()));
		helper.runAfterDelay(14, () -> {
			ArenaFight.giveInHand(arena.kid, tier);
			ArenaFight.aimAt(arena.kid, arena.hisMiddle());
			arena.fight.throwBack(arena.kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
		});
	}
}
```

```java
	/**
	 * A throw-back hit him mid-yeet: he hangs in the air, spinning and flailing, until {@link #unfreeze} lets his flight
	 * go on or a new yeet replaces it.
	 */
	public void freeze() {
		if (frozen || yeet == null || finale) {
			return;
		}
		frozen = true;
		frozenVelocity = getDeltaMovement();
		frozenFloating = isNoGravity();
		setNoGravity(true);
		setDeltaMovement(Vec3.ZERO);
		armsUp(true);
		playSound(SoundEvents.EVOKER_HURT, 1.5F, 0.7F);
	}

	/** He is let go: the flight goes on where it stopped, so he falls the normal way. */
	public void unfreeze() {
		if (!frozen) {
			return;
		}
		frozen = false;
		setNoGravity(frozenFloating);
		setDeltaMovement(frozenVelocity);
	}

	public boolean isFrozen() {
		return frozen;
	}

	private void hang(ServerLevel level) {
		spin(40F);
		setDeltaMovement(Vec3.ZERO);
		level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CLOUD, getX(), getY() + 1, getZ(), 2, 0.3, 0.6, 0.3, 0.02);
	}
```

and `frozen = false;` as the first statement of `yeet(Vec3, double, YeetStyle, Vec3, Traps)`, `finalYeet()` and `comeBack(...)`.

- [ ] **Step 4: The fight freezes him, waits, and lets him go**

`Terugbommen`: change `tick` to take whether the fight is paused (a juggle holds every timer, the lifetime of a Terugbom included, but a kid can still catch one):

```java
	/** Once a tick: the ones that are over poof (not while a juggle holds the fight), and a kid who walks onto one they have room for catches it. */
	void tick(List<ServerPlayer> kids, BiConsumer<ServerPlayer, BombTier> caught, boolean paused) {
		for (Iterator<Lying> it = lying.iterator(); it.hasNext(); ) {
			Lying bomb = it.next();
			if (!bomb.item.isAlive()) {
				it.remove();
				continue;
			}
			if (!paused && --bomb.ticksLeft <= 0) {
				poof(bomb);
				it.remove();
				continue;
			}
			for (ServerPlayer kid : kids) {
				if (reaches(kid, bomb.item) && Ammo.canCatch(carried(kid)) && give(client, kid, bomb.tier)) {
					bomb.item.discard();
					it.remove();
					caught.accept(kid, bomb.tier);
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}
```

```java
	/** Whether a juggle question is open: nothing in the fight times out then (R1.4). */

	}
```

Replace the first part of `tick()` (everything before `phaseTicks--;`) by:

```java
	void tick() {
		if (phase == Phase.OVER) {
			return;
		}
		if (!boss.isAlive() && phase != Phase.FINALE) {
			end();
			return;
		}
		// While a juggle question is open nothing in the fight times out (R1.4): he hangs there, however long the kid thinks.
		boolean frozen = juggleOpen();
		if (!frozen && arena != null && phase != Phase.FINALE && boss.getY() < arena.minY()) {
			flewOut();
		}

		participants.update();
		if (arena != null && config.noBuildInFight) {
			modes.update(level.getServer(), participants.players());
		}
		recordMotions();
		showRings();
		if (dodging()) {
			terugbommen.tick(participants.players(), this::caught, frozen);
			for (ServerPlayer kid : participants.players()) {
				Terugbommen.returnFromMenu(sessions.client(), kid);
			}
			pickUpTouchedBombs();
		}
		if (frozen) {
			return;
		}
		phaseTicks--;
```

(the `switch (phase)` that follows stays).

Replace `backReached`'s tail and `bounces`, add `juggle` and the flag:

```java
	/** The flight he is on was juggled already (or is a super yeet): a second throw-back in it drops back. */
	private boolean flightJuggled;
```

```java
	public void backReached(UUID throwerId, BombTier tier) {
		ServerPlayer thrower = level.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayer(throwerId);
		if (bounces(thrower)) {
			dropBack(tier);
		} else if (boss.isFlying()) {
			juggle(thrower, tier);
		} else {
			tally(throwerId).backHit();
			boss.stagger(Pacing.STAGGER_TICKS);
			stagger.hit();
			if (ask(thrower, tier, boss.blockPosition(), QuestionKind.TERUG) == null) {
				BombEffects.fizzle(level, boss.blockPosition());
			}
		}
	}

	/**
	 * Why a throw-back can't hit him now: he is beaten, staggered or landing, the kid who threw it is answering something
	 * else, has left or has died, or he flies from a yeet that was juggled already (a super yeet included) or hangs in a
	 * juggle now (two kids at once: the first throw-back counts).
	 */
	private boolean bounces(@Nullable ServerPlayer thrower) {
		return thrower == null || !thrower.isAlive() || sessions.isBusy(thrower) || !participants.players().contains(thrower)
				|| decided() || boss.isStaggered() || boss.isFinale()
				|| boss.isReacting() && !boss.isFlying()
				|| boss.isFlying() && (flightJuggled || juggleOpen());
	}

	/** A throw-back hit him in mid-yeet: he freezes, and the question comes while he hangs there. */
	private void juggle(ServerPlayer thrower, BombTier tier) {
		boss.freeze();
		if (ask(thrower, tier, boss.blockPosition(), QuestionKind.JUGGLE) == null) {
			boss.unfreeze();
			BombEffects.fizzle(level, boss.blockPosition());
			return;
		}
		flightJuggled = true;
		FightTally tally = tally(thrower.getUUID());
		tally.backHit();
		tally.juggle();
	}
```

```java
	@Override
	public void onEnded(UUID player, QuizSession session) {
		QuestionKind kind = open.remove(player);
		if (kind == QuestionKind.JUGGLE) {
			// Timed out, died or disconnected: he falls the normal way.
			boss.unfreeze();
		}
		// ANSWERING also notices by itself that nobody is busy; doing it now spares the kids a tick of waiting.
		if (phase == Phase.ANSWERING && player.equals(currentTarget)) {
			cooldown(COOLDOWN_TICKS);
		}
	}
```

Replace `onKickHit` by its final form (a super yeet's chickens come down at the floor, it has its own sound and sparks, and it is the end of a juggle):

```java
	void onKickHit(Vec3 where, BombTier tier, @Nullable Kick kick) {
		if (decided()) {
			return;
		}
		YeetKind kind = kick == null ? YeetKind.KICK : kick.kind();
		// The bigger the bomb, the bigger the bang and the further he flies.
		boom(new Vec3(boss.getX(), boss.getY() + 1, boss.getZ()), tier);
		if (tier == BombTier.KIPPENBOM && config.kippenbomChickens) {
			// A juggle's chickens come down at the arena floor, not in the air where he hangs.
			Vec3 chickensAt = kind == YeetKind.SUPER ? floorBelow(boss.position()).add(0, 1, 0) : boss.position();
			if (config.countingKippenbom && kick != null) {
				sessions.countChickens(level, chickensAt, kick.fact(), "fight");
			} else {
				BombEffects.flyingChickens(level, chickensAt);
				sessions.collectSpectacles(level, BlockPos.containing(chickensAt));
			}
		}
		if (hit()) {
			return;
		}
		if (kind == YeetKind.SUPER) {
			// The super yeet's own sound and sparks.
			level.playSound(null, boss.getX(), boss.getY(), boss.getZ(), SoundEvents.FIREWORK_ROCKET_BLAST, SoundSource.HOSTILE, 2.0F, 1.0F);
			level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.FIREWORK, boss.getX(), boss.getY() + 1, boss.getZ(), 80, 0.8, 0.8, 0.8, 0.2);
		}
		ServerPlayer kicker = kick == null ? null : level.getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayer(kick.kicker());
		Vec3 kickedFrom = kicker != null ? kicker.position() : where;
		yeetKick = kick;
		yeetFactor = kick == null ? 1.0 : kick.factor(config.yeetBoost);
		double power = YeetPower.power(kind, 0.9 + level.getRandom().nextDouble() * 0.5, tier.yeetPower(), yeetFactor);
		reactionCooldown = COOLDOWN_TICKS;
		// A super yeet is the end of a juggle: it can't be juggled again. Every other new flight can be, once.
		flightJuggled = kind == YeetKind.SUPER;
		// A kicked bomb sends him away from where it hit; a throw-back or a super yeet from the kid who threw it.
		boss.yeet(kind == YeetKind.KICK ? where : kickedFrom, power, reactions.nextFlight(arena != null, config.yeetCatalogue), kickedFrom, traps());
		phase = Phase.REACTING;
		phaseTicks = REACTION_MIN_TICKS;
	}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing GameTests**

`src/gametest/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/gametest/TerugbomLeakGameTest.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.gametest;

import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTestHelper;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;

import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.gametest.v1.GameTest;

import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.config.TafelBoemConfig;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.BombTier;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.item.TerugbomItem;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.registry.ModItems;

/**
 * R1.4's load contract for Terugbommen: they exist only in a running arena fight. When it ends they poof on the
 * ground and in the pockets; a kid who leaves or dies loses theirs (the kids keep their inventory on death, so
 * they are taken out, not dropped); and one a kid has on login is removed.
 */
public class TerugbomLeakGameTest {
	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void whenTheFightEndsTheGroundAndThePocketsPoof(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		ServerPlayer sibling = arena.addKid(arena.box.middleX() + 8, arena.box.middleZ() + 8);
		pocket(arena.kid, 4);
		pocket(sibling, Inventory.SLOT_OFFHAND);
		arena.fight.dropTerugbom(arena.kid.position().add(-8, 0, -8), BombTier.GEWONE_TNT);
		helper.runAfterDelay(3, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().size() == 1 && carried(arena.kid) == 1 && carried(sibling) == 1, "before: one on the ground, one in each pocket");
			arena.end();
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().isEmpty(), "after: none on the ground");
			helper.assertTrue(carried(arena.kid) == 0 && carried(sibling) == 0, "and none in a pocket, the offhand included");
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void aKidWhoLeavesTheArenaLosesTheirs(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		pocket(arena.kid, 2);
		arena.fight.dropTerugbom(arena.kid.position().add(-8, 0, -8), BombTier.KNALLETJE);
		helper.runAfterDelay(3, () -> arena.kid.teleportTo(arena.box.minX() - 15, arena.floorY, arena.box.middleZ()));
		helper.runAfterDelay(8, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(carried(arena.kid) == 0, "their Terugbom is gone from their pocket");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().size() == 1, "the one on the floor stays: the fight goes on");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 60, padding = 48)
	public void aKidWhoDiesLosesTheirsAndNothingDrops(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		ArenaFight.survival(arena.kid);
		pocket(arena.kid, 2);
		helper.runAfterDelay(3, () -> arena.kid.kill(arena.level));
		helper.runAfterDelay(12, () -> {
			helper.assertTrue(arena.kid.isDeadOrDying(), "the kid died");
			helper.assertTrue(carried(arena.kid) == 0, "their Terugbom is taken out of their pocket");
			helper.assertTrue(arena.terugbommen().isEmpty(), "and not dropped on the floor");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	@GameTest(maxTicks = 40, padding = 48)
	public void aTerugbomInAPocketOnLoginIsRemoved(GameTestHelper helper) {
		ArenaFight arena = new ArenaFight(helper, new TafelBoemConfig());
		// Not in the fight: a kid who logs in with one, saved there when the game stopped.
		ServerPlayer saved = arena.addKid(arena.box.middleX() + 15, arena.box.middleZ());
		pocket(saved, 3);
		pocket(arena.kid, 3);
		helper.runAfterDelay(3, () -> {
			arena.fights.onJoin(saved);
			helper.assertTrue(carried(saved) == 0, "removed on login");
			arena.fights.onJoin(arena.kid);
			helper.assertTrue(carried(arena.kid) == 1, "a kid who is in the running fight keeps theirs");
			arena.end();
			helper.succeed();
		});
	}

	private static void pocket(ServerPlayer kid, int slot) {
		kid.getInventory().setItem(slot, new ItemStack(ModItems.terugbom(BombTier.KNALLETJE)));
	}

	private static int carried(ServerPlayer kid) {
		int count = 0;
		for (int slot = 0; slot < Inventory.INVENTORY_SIZE; slot++) {
			count += kid.getInventory().getItem(slot).getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem ? 1 : 0;
		}
		return count + (kid.getInventory().getItem(Inventory.SLOT_OFFHAND).getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem ? 1 : 0);
	}
}
```

Register `"nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.gametest.TerugbomLeakGameTest"` in `fabric.mod.json`, after `JuggleGameTest`.

`Terugbommen`: add (imports as there; `net.minecraft.world.inventory.AbstractContainerMenu` is not needed):

```java
	/**
	 * Takes every Terugbom out of this kid's pockets, the offhand and the cursor: not dropped, just gone (the kids keep
	 * their inventory on death, so a dying kid's would otherwise stay with them).
	 */
	static void strip(ServerPlayer kid) {
		Inventory inventory = kid.getInventory();
		for (int slot = 0; slot < Inventory.INVENTORY_SIZE; slot++) {
			if (inventory.getItem(slot).getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem) {
				inventory.setItem(slot, ItemStack.EMPTY);
			}
		}
		if (inventory.getItem(Inventory.SLOT_OFFHAND).getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem) {
			inventory.setItem(Inventory.SLOT_OFFHAND, ItemStack.EMPTY);
		}
		if (kid.containerMenu.getCarried().getItem() instanceof TerugbomItem) {
			kid.containerMenu.setCarried(ItemStack.EMPTY);
		}
	}
```

### Task 12: The evaluation's logs, checked end to end

The log items were each written with their feature: the fight line's counts (Tasks 3, 6, 7, 8), the throw line (Task 6), the answer log's contexts (`terug` and `juggle`, from `QuestionKind`, Tasks 5, 8, 10) and the yeet line's marks (Task 9). Playtest 6 is judged from those lines alone, so one test plays a throw-back through a whole TafelBoem world and reads them back.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/gametest/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/gametest/LogsGameTest.java`
- Modify: `src/gametest/resources/fabric.mod.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above; `TestWorld` (answers every question right at once, `log` is the fights log, `sessions.learners().log()` the answer log).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

`src/gametest/java/nl/jeeninga/tafelboem/gametest/LogsGameTest.java`:

```java
package nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.gametest;

import java.util.List;

import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTestHelper;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;

import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.gametest.v1.GameTest;

import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.boss.BossFight;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.AnswerRecord;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.BombTier;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.FightLevel;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.FightRecord;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.core.YeetKind;
import nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.world.ArenaRun;

/**
 * Playtest 6 is judged from the game's log lines, so the numbers must really get there: a Terugbom caught, thrown
 * back and answered right shows in the fight line's counts, in the answer log's context and in the yeet line's mark.
 */
public class LogsGameTest {
	@GameTest(maxTicks = 600, padding = TestWorld.PADDING)
	@Fight
	public void aCaughtThrownBackAndAnsweredTerugbomEndsUpInTheLogs(GameTestHelper helper) {
		TestWorld test = new TestWorld(helper).running();
		ServerPlayer kid = test.kidInTheHub();
		test.world.enterDoor(kid, 1);
		BossFight[] seen = {null};
		int[] step = {0};
		helper.onEachTick(() -> {
			BossFight fight = test.world.run(FightLevel.MAKKELIJK).map(ArenaRun::fight).orElse(null);
			if (fight == null) {
				return;
			}
			seen[0] = fight;
			if (step[0] == 0) {
				// He is there: a Terugbom drops next to the kid, who catches it (the selected slot, the hand).
				fight.dropTerugbom(kid.position().add(1, 0, 0), BombTier.KNALLETJE);
				step[0] = 1;
			} else if (step[0] == 1 && fight.tally(kid.getUUID()).caught() == 1) {
				ArenaFight.aimAt(kid, fight.boss().position().add(0, fight.boss().getBbHeight() / 2, 0));
				fight.throwBack(kid, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
				step[0] = 2;
			} else if (step[0] == 2 && fight.tally(kid.getUUID()).terugRight() == 1 && fight.lastYeet() != null) {
				// TestWorld answered the Terug! question right; his flight is over. The exit door writes the kid's fight line.
				step[0] = 3;
				test.world.enterDoor(kid, 0);
			}
		});

		helper.succeedWhen(() -> {
			helper.assertTrue(step[0] == 3, "wait for the throw-back, its answer and his landing");
			List<FightRecord> fights = test.log.read(kid.getPlainTextName());
			helper.assertTrue(fights.size() == 1, "the exit door wrote the fight line: " + fights);
			FightRecord line = fights.getFirst();
			helper.assertTrue(line.caught() == 1 && line.thrownBack() == 1 && line.backHits() == 1 && line.juggles() == 0, "the counts: " + line.describe());
			helper.assertTrue(line.terugRight() == 1 && line.terugWrong() == 0, "the Terug! answers: " + line.describe());
			helper.assertTrue(line.describe().contains("caught 1, threw back 1 (1 hit), Terug! 1 right 0 wrong"), "and the line says so: " + line.describe());
			helper.assertTrue(seen[0].lastYeet().kind() == YeetKind.BACK, "the yeet line marks the throw-back: " + seen[0].lastYeet().describe());
			helper.assertTrue(seen[0].lastYeet().high() || line.farthestBack() > 0, "and the fight line has the farthest throw-back: " + line.describe());
			List<AnswerRecord> answers = test.sessions.learners().log().read(kid.getPlainTextName());
			helper.assertTrue(answers.stream().anyMatch(answer -> answer.context().equals("terug") && answer.correct() && answer.millis() >= 0),
					"the answer log has the sum with its time and the context terug: " + answers);
			test.close();
		});
	}
}
```

Register `"nl.jeeninga.tafelboem.gametest.LogsGameTest"` in `fabric.mod.json`, after `TerugbomLeakGameTest`.

```markdown
- **No building (1.4).** Graaf Fout comes about three seconds after you walk in. The building chest,
  the bell and the traps are still in the mod behind the switches `buildPhase` and `traps`, both off.
  While the fight runs you are in adventure mode, so nobody breaks or places blocks; you are back in

  only gets you when it lands within 3 blocks of you, and a ring of dust shows where he thinks it
  will land. A bomb that misses drops as a glowing Terugbom (he says "Mis!"): walk onto it to catch
  it (3 at most), right-click to throw it back. It homes in on him when it gets near and passes
  through everyone else. A hit makes him wobble and skip a throw, and you get a Terug! question: right
  is a hit and a harder yeet, wrong fizzles like any wrong answer. Hit him while he flies and he
  freezes in the air until you have answered: right is a super yeet. Terugbommen only exist in a
  fight, and poof after 15 seconds on the floor.
```

3. In the switches table: change the `buildPhase` row's default to `false` and its text ("`true` is the 1.2.0 build phase … ; the default since 1.4 is `false`: a random arena, and he comes 3 seconds after the first kid"), change `dodging`'s text to "A bomb that lands more than 3 blocks from the kid it was thrown at drops as a Terugbom; he aims ahead of a running kid and throws quicker; a ring shows where it lands. `false` is 1.2.0: wherever it lands, that kid answers", change `traps`'s default to `false`, and add after `arenaSafety`:

```markdown
| `noBuildInFight` | `true` | Kids in an arena with a running fight are in adventure mode: no breaking or placing. Their own mode comes back when it ends, they leave, die or log in again |
| `landingRing` | `true` | A ring of dust shows where his bomb will land |
| `dodgeLead1`, `dodgeLead2`, `dodgeLead3` | `0.70`, `0.85`, `0.95` | How much of a running kid's movement he aims ahead, on levels 1, 2 and 3 (0 to 1) |
| `dodgeFlight1`, `dodgeFlight2`, `dodgeFlight3` | `0.85`, `0.75`, `0.68` | His bomb's flight time as a part of the old one, on levels 1, 2 and 3; smaller is quicker and harder |
| `dodgeMinFlightTicks` | `12` | No bomb flies shorter than this many ticks (20 a second) |
```

4. In *Other worlds*, change "Dodging, lying bombs and traps belong to the arenas behind the level doors" to "Dodging, Terugbommen and traps belong to the arenas behind the level doors".

5. Under the roadmap's screenshot tables add the three screenshots (the files there start with a running number: copy them first, `cp build/run/clientGameTest/screenshots/*_r1.4-01-landing-ring.png docs/screenshots/r1.4-landing-ring.png`, and likewise `*_r1.4-02-juggle.png` to `r1.4-juggle.png` and `*_r1.4-03-terugbom-in-hand.png` to `r1.4-terugbom-in-hand.png`):

```markdown
| Landing ring | The juggle question | A Terugbom in hand |
|---|---|---|
| ![Ring of dust where his bomb will land](docs/screenshots/r1.4-landing-ring.png) | ![The question while Graaf Fout hangs frozen](docs/screenshots/r1.4-juggle.png) | ![A caught Terugbom](docs/screenshots/r1.4-terugbom-in-hand.png) |
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `docs/evaluations/R1.4.md`**

```markdown
# R1.4 "Terug!": evaluation

Release 1.4.0. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-tafelboem-design.md`, *R1.4 "Terug!"*.
Decided with the parent on 2026-11-13, after the playtest of 1.3.0 (`R1.2.md`, `R1.3.md`). The kids
didn't like building; dodging was too easy, and the bombs left lying around were no fun.

## Hypothesis

Dodging that is hard but pays off with a throw-back makes the fight more fun and gives more sums per
minute, not fewer.

Learning rule: a dodge never takes health off Graaf Fout by itself. It earns an extra chance at a
hit, and that hit still needs a right answer.

## Go when

- dodged throws at a free kid, both kids pooled, are 20–45% on level 1 and 10–30% on level 3
  (`dodged` over `thrown at` in the fight lines, per `level`; the `TafelBoem throw:` lines have the
  distance, the flight and the kid's speed behind each)
- at least 4 answers per minute in the fight (1.2.0 reached 4.7), and % right and "?" no worse than 1.2.0
- the kids catch and throw back without being told, and at least some throw-backs hit
- the kids still ask to play

## Watch

- how often the kids juggle, and whether they try to (`juggled` in the fight line)
- a kid who hoards bombs instead of throwing (`caught` against `threw back`)
- one sibling catching most of the bombs
- throw-backs that keep missing: is the aim help enough? (`threw back` against `hit`; the spec's aim
  is that the younger kid hits a standing Graaf Fout at least half the time)
- whether the ring makes it too easy (it has a switch, `landingRing`)
- deaths on Normal (`died`)
- whether adventure mode in the fight bothers them (`noBuildInFight`)
- the ring is drawn at 2.8 blocks and the zone is 3.0: a kid standing between the two is hit
  although outside the ring; does it look like cheating?
- the yeet power cap (`YeetPower.MAX_POWER`): do throw-back yeets on level 3 feel no bigger than kicks?
- two kids at once: a juggle freezes every timer in the fight, including the other kid's questions' pause

## Check by hand once

Things the GameTests can't see: right-clicking a Terugbom in a real client throws it; Q does
nothing; a Terugbom dragged into a chest comes back; the hint appears once; nobody can build in a
fight and everybody can again after it; the ring and the glow look right in daylight.

## Playtest 6

Not played yet. Numbers from the `TafelBoem fight:`, `throw:`, `yeet:` and `session:` lines and the
answer log (context `terug` and `juggle`). The baseline is playtest 4 and 5 (`R1.2.md`, `R1.3.md`).
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The baseline numbers**

The spec says to add the 1.3.0 playtest's log numbers to `R1.2.md` and `R1.3.md` first, as the baseline. They come from the parent's `latest.log` (see *Playtests* in `AGENTS.md`), which is not in the repository. If the parent has shared it (a `logshare.in` link, read as `AGENTS.md` describes), add under each file's *Playtest* heading the numbers of the `TafelBoem fight:`, `yeet:`, `build:` and `session:` lines that file's plan lists. **Never invent them.** Without the log, leave both files as they are and say in the hand-over that the baseline is still missing.

- [ ] **Step 5: Verify everything, then hand over**

```bash
./gradlew build
./gradlew runGameTest
./gradlew runClientGameTest
./gradlew runFightGameTests -Ptests='tafelboem-test:*' -Prepeat=3   # several minutes
```

Expected: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` three times, "All N required tests passed" in the second and fourth. Report each result with its numbers; don't claim a pass you didn't see. `git diff main --stat` and `git diff main | grep -n -E '192[.]168|/hom[e]/'` must show nothing (the brackets keep this line from matching itself); read the added docs once for names (the kids are "Kid A" and "Kid B", the user "the parent").

Then stop. Tell the parent branch `r1.4-terug` is ready, with the test results, the three screenshot paths and whether the baseline numbers are in. Merging, tagging `v1.4.0` and `publishPack` happen only after the parent's go, through a cheap subagent with a strict brief (the parent's standing rule).

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**

```bash
git add AGENTS.md README.md docs/evaluations/R1.4.md docs/screenshots/r1.4-landing-ring.png docs/screenshots/r1.4-terugbom-in-hand.png \
	docs/screenshots/r1.4-juggle.png
git commit -m "Write down R1.4 for the kids, the parent and playtest 6

The README tells the kids how to dodge, catch and throw back and lists the new
and changed switches; AGENTS.md gets the Terugbom rows of the load contract, the
adventure-mode rule and the 1.4.0 log lines; playtest 6 has its evaluation page
with the hypothesis, the go bar and what to watch.

Co-Authored-By: <the model that wrote this commit>"
```

(add `docs/evaluations/R1.2.md docs/evaluations/R1.3.md` to the `git add` only when Step 4 changed them).
